              'class ChatScreen extends StatefulWidget {\n'
              '  final Chat chat;\n'
              '\n'
              '  /// When set (e.g. opened from search), the chat scrolls to and briefly\n'
              '  /// highlights this message after it opens.\n'
              '  final String? initialMessageId;\n'
              '\n'
              '  /// When set, this is a THREAD under that message rather than the room:\n'
              '  /// the transcript is the root plus its replies, and anything sent joins\n'
              '  /// them instead of the main conversation.\n'
              '  ///\n'
              '  /// The same screen rather than a new one, so a thread gets the composer,\n'
              '  /// the attachments, reactions, editing and delivery exactly as the room\n'
              '  /// has them — a second, thinner chat screen would drift from this one the\n'
              '  /// first time either changed.\n'
              '  final String? threadRootId;\n'
              '\n'
              '  const ChatScreen({\n'
              '    super.key,\n'
              '    required this.chat,\n'
              '    this.initialMessageId,\n'
              '    this.threadRootId,\n'
              '  });\n'
              '\n'

              '\n'
              '  /// Id of the newest incoming message we\'ve sent a read receipt for, so a\n'
              '  /// receipt is sent once per new message (not on every store change).\n'
              '  String? _lastAckedIncomingId;\n'
              '\n'
              '  /// Presence: whether the peer is currently online, plus timers to broadcast\n'
              '  /// our own presence and to revert the peer to offline after a quiet period.\n'
              '  /// [_peerWhere] is what their freshest ping said: \'chat\' means they are in\n'
              '  /// THIS conversation right now, \'app\' means the app is open elsewhere.\n'
              '  bool _peerOnline = false;\n'
              '  String _peerWhere = \'chat\';\n'
              '  Timer? _presenceSend;\n'
              '  Timer? _presenceRevert;\n'
              '\n'
              '  /// The unread count when the chat was opened, and the id of the message the\n'
              '  /// "unread messages" divider should sit above (captured before markRead).\n'
              '  int _initialUnread = 0;\n'
              '  String? _unreadAnchorId;\n'
              '\n'
              '  /// Per-message keys (for scroll-to-message) and the id currently flashing\n'
              '  /// after a reply-quote jump.\n'
              '  final Map<String, GlobalKey> _messageKeys = {};\n'
              '  String? _highlightedId;\n'
              '\n'
              '  /// Deferred scroll-to-message when opened from search, and the timer that\n'
              '  /// clears a jumped-to message\'s highlight.\n'
              '  Timer? _jumpTimer;\n'
              '  Timer? _highlightClear;\n'
              '\n'
              '  String get _chatId => widget.chat.id;\n'
              '\n'
              '  @override\n'
              '  void initState() {\n'
              '    super.initState();\n'
              '    // Make sure a store entry exists (e.g. for a freshly started chat).\n'
              '    _store.upsert(widget.chat);\n'
              '    _captureUnreadAnchor();\n'
              '    _scrollController.addListener(_onScroll);\n'
              '    // Watch the keyboard: when it opens it eats the bottom of the transcript,\n'
              '    // so the latest message you were reading (or just typed) slides behind it.\n'
              '    // didChangeMetrics re-pins to the end when the inset changes.\n'
              '    WidgetsBinding.instance.addObserver(this);\n'
              '    _store.addListener(_refreshSuggestions);\n'
              '    _refreshSuggestions();\n'
              '    // Follow the conversation: an arriving message scrolls into view when\n'
              '    // you are already reading the end of the transcript (your own sends\n'
              '    // handle themselves in _deliver).\n'
              '    _followCount = _messages.length;\n'
              '    _store.addListener(_maybeFollowNewMessage);\n'
              '    _store.addListener(_maybeBuzzOnPoke);\n'

              '    // arrival (or predates this visit) — opening must not re-buzz it.\n'

              '    RelayService.instance.screenshotPing.addListener(_onRemoteScreenshot);\n'
              '    RelayService.instance.recordingPing.addListener(_onRemoteRecording);\n'
              '    RelayService.instance.ghostShotPing.addListener(_onRemoteGhostShot);\n'
              '    // A push for the conversation you are reading should not draw a banner\n'
              '    // over the message the app has already put on screen.\n'
              '    PushService.instance.setOpenChat(widget.chat.contact.phone);\n'
              '    // The presence answer-pong reads this: while THIS chat is on screen, an\n'
              '    // incoming ping from its contact needs no "online elsewhere" answer —\n'
              '    // the periodic chat ping above it is already saying something stronger.\n'
              '    if (!widget.chat.contact.isGroup) {\n'
              '      RelayService.instance.openChatDigits =\n'
              '          RelayService.digits(widget.chat.contact.phone);\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    // When opened from search, jump to the matched message once it\'s laid out.\n'
              '    if (widget.initialMessageId != null) {\n'
              '      WidgetsBinding.instance.addPostFrameCallback((_) {\n'

              '        _jumpTimer = Timer(const Duration(milliseconds: 250),\n'
              '            () => _jumpToMessage(widget.initialMessageId!));\n'
              '      });\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    // Listening costs nothing and needs no relay — the notifier is local.\n'
              '    // Ungated so the header logic is the same code (and the same tests) with\n'
              '    // and without a configured relay; only the SEND side below needs one.\n'
              '    if (_isRealPeer(widget.chat.contact)) {\n'
              '      RelayService.instance.presencePing.addListener(_onPresencePing);\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    if (RelayConfig.isEnabled) {\n'
              '      RelayService.instance.typingPing.addListener(_onTypingPing);\n'
              '      if (_isRealPeer(widget.chat.contact)) {\n'
              '        _store.addListener(_maybeSendReadReceipt);\n'
              '        // Announce we\'re here now, then keep announcing while the chat is\n'
              '        // open (unless the user has hidden their online status). The first\n'
              '        // announce waits a frame: _broadcastPresence asks the route whether\n'
              '        // it is current, and ModalRoute.of cannot be asked from initState.\n'
              '        WidgetsBinding.instance\n'
              '            .addPostFrameCallback((_) => _broadcastPresence());\n'
              '        _presenceSend = Timer.periodic(\n'
              '          const Duration(seconds: 15),\n'
              '          (_) => _broadcastPresence(),\n'
              '        );\n'
              '      }\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    WidgetsBinding.instance.addPostFrameCallback((_) {\n'
              '      _store.markRead(_chatId);\n'
              '      _maybeSendReadReceipt();\n'
              '      // Opened from search, the matched message is the destination — the\n'
              '      // opening jump would fight the jump to the match.\n'
              '      if (widget.initialMessageId == null) _jumpToBottom();\n'
              '      // X\'s rule: the recovery code gets squared away before the first\n'
              '      // message — offered here, enforced at send. Not in your own notes;\n'
              '      // there is nothing pairwise to protect.\n'
              '      if (!_isNoteToSelf && mounted) maybePromptRecoverySetup(context);\n'
              '    });\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  /// The transcript length last seen, so the follow listener can tell a new\n'
              '  /// message from any other store change (a tick advancing, an edit).\n'
              '  int _followCount = 0;\n'
              '\n'
              '  /// Scrolls an ARRIVING message into view — only when the view is already\n'
              '  /// at (or within a screen of) the end. Somebody who scrolled up to read\n'
              '  /// is left where they are; the down-arrow button is how they come back.\n'
              '  void _maybeFollowNewMessage() {\n'
              '    final count = _messages.length;\n'
              '    if (count == _followCount) return;\n'
              '    final grew = count > _followCount;\n'
              '    _followCount = count;\n'
              '    if (!grew || !_scrollController.hasClients) return;\n'
              '    final distance =\n'
              '        _scrollController.position.maxScrollExtent - _scrollController.offset;\n'
              '    if (distance < 320) {\n'
              '      WidgetsBinding.instance.addPostFrameCallback((_) => _animateToBottom());\n'
              '    }\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  /// Sends a \'read\' receipt to a real peer when a new incoming message appears\n'
              '  /// while this chat is open (once per message, so no receipt ping-pong).\n'
              '  void _maybeSendReadReceipt() {\n'
              '    if (!AppState.sendReadReceipts.value) return;\n'
              '    // A request tells its sender nothing — not even that it was read —\n'
              '    // until it is accepted. Read live so accepting mid-screen lifts it.\n'
              '    if (_store.chatById(_chatId)?.isRequest ?? false) return;\n'
              '    final incoming =\n'
              '        _store.chatById(_chatId)?.messages.where((m) => !m.isMe).toList();\n'
              '    if (incoming == null || incoming.isEmpty) return;\n'
              '    final lastId = incoming.last.id;\n'
              '    if (lastId == _lastAckedIncomingId) return;\n'
              '    final phones = _relayPhones();\n'
              '    if (phones.isEmpty) return;\n'
              '    _lastAckedIncomingId = lastId;\n'
              '    for (final phone in phones) {\n'
              '      RelayService.instance.sendReceipt(phone, \'read\', messageId: lastId);\n'
              '    }\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  /// Broadcasts that we\'re typing (throttled) to whoever this chat reaches.\n'
              '  void _onTyping() {\n'
              '    // Respect the privacy setting: don\'t leak "typing…" when it\'s off.\n'
              '    if (!AppState.sendTypingIndicators.value) return;\n'
              '    // Typing in an unaccepted request leaks that you saw it. Sending the\n'
              '    // reply is the moment the conversation is accepted, not composing it.\n'
              '    if (_store.chatById(_chatId)?.isRequest ?? false) return;\n'
              '    final now = DateTime.now();\n'
              '    if (_lastTypingSent != null &&\n'
              '        now.difference(_lastTypingSent!) < const Duration(seconds: 2)) {\n'
              '      return;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    final phones = _relayPhones();\n'
              '    if (phones.isEmpty) return;\n'
              '    _lastTypingSent = now;\n'
              '    for (final phone in phones) {\n'
              '      RelayService.instance.sendTyping(phone);\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    // The Snapchat move: a closed app finds out someone STARTED typing to\n'
              '    // them. Once per minute at most — the live pings above carry the\n'
              '    // moment-to-moment state for an open app; this is one nudge, not a\n'
              '    // keystroke feed. 1:1 only: a group where everyone\'s phone buzzes for\n'
              '    // every composer is a group people mute.\n'
              '    if (!widget.chat.contact.isGroup &&\n'
              '        (_lastTypingPush == null ||\n'
              '            now.difference(_lastTypingPush!) > const Duration(minutes: 1))) {\n'
              '      _lastTypingPush = now;\n'
              '      final myName = AppState.profile.value.name;\n'
              '      PushService.instance.notify(widget.chat.contact.phone,\n'
              '          title: myName.isEmpty ? \'Typing…\' : myName, body: \'Typing…\');\n'
              '    }\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  DateTime? _lastTypingPush;\n'
              '\n'
              '  /// Shows the typing indicator when someone in *this* chat is typing — the\n'
              '  /// peer of a 1:1 conversation, or any member of a group (who gets named).\n'
              '  void _onTypingPing() {\n'
              '    final fromDigits = RelayService.instance.typingFromDigits;\n'
              '    if (fromDigits == null) return;\n'
              '    String name = \'\';\n'
              '    if (widget.chat.contact.isGroup) {\n'
              '      final members = _store.chatById(_chatId)?.members ?? const [];\n'
              '      final match = members.where(\n'
              '          (m) => RelayService.digits(m.phone) == fromDigits).toList();\n'
              '      if (match.isEmpty) return;\n'
              '      name = match.first.name.split(\' \').first;\n'
              '    } else if (fromDigits != RelayService.digits(widget.chat.contact.phone)) {\n'
              '      return;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    if (!mounted) return;\n'
              '    setState(() {\n'
              '      _isTyping = true;\n'
              '      _typingName = name;\n'
              '    });\n'
              '    _typingClear?.cancel();\n'
              '    _typingClear = Timer(const Duration(seconds: 3), () {\n'
              '      if (mounted) setState(() => _isTyping = false);\n'
              '    });\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  void _broadcastPresence() {\n'
              '    if (!AppState.shareLastSeen.value) return;\n'
              '    // A chat buried under another route is NOT being looked at, and its\n'
              '    // ping would say it is — the same reason a screenshot of the chat list\n'
              '    // isn\'t announced as one of the conversation. The check also self-heals\n'
              '    // openChatDigits after a covering chat\'s dispose cleared it, so the\n'
              '    // answer-pong logic never suppresses (or answers) for the wrong peer.\n'
              '    if (!mounted || !(ModalRoute.of(context)?.isCurrent ?? true)) return;\n'
              '    RelayService.instance.openChatDigits =\n'
              '        RelayService.digits(widget.chat.contact.phone);\n'
              '    // Presence is "I am here, looking at our chat" — exactly the thing an\n'
              '    // unaccepted request must not learn. Checked live on every tick.\n'
              '    if (_store.chatById(_chatId)?.isRequest ?? false) return;\n'
              '    RelayService.instance.sendPresence(widget.chat.contact.phone);\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  /// Marks the peer online when their presence ping arrives, reverting to\n'
              '  /// offline after a quiet period.\n'
              '  void _onPresencePing() {\n'
              '    if (RelayService.instance.presenceFromDigits !=\n'
              '        RelayService.digits(widget.chat.contact.phone)) {\n'
              '      return;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    if (!mounted) return;\n'
              '    final where = RelayService.instance.presenceWhere;\n'
              '    if (!_peerOnline || _peerWhere != where) {\n'
              '      setState(() {\n'
              '        _peerOnline = true;\n'
              '        _peerWhere = where;\n'
              '      });\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    _presenceRevert?.cancel();\n'
              '    _presenceRevert = Timer(const Duration(seconds: 35), () {\n'
              '      if (mounted) setState(() => _peerOnline = false);\n'
              '    });\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  /// Records where the "unread messages" divider goes: above the first of the\n'
              '  /// last [unreadCount] incoming messages.\n'
              '  void _captureUnreadAnchor() {\n'
              '    final chat = _store.chatById(_chatId);\n'
              '    final unread = chat?.unreadCount ?? 0;\n'
              '    if (unread <= 0) return;\n'
              '    final incoming = chat!.messages.where((m) => !m.isMe).toList();\n'
              '    if (unread <= incoming.length) {\n'
              '      _initialUnread = unread;\n'
              '      _unreadAnchorId = incoming[incoming.length - unread].id;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  void _onScroll() {\n'
              '    if (!_scrollController.hasClients) return;\n'
              '    final distance =\n'
              '        _scrollController.position.maxScrollExtent - _scrollController.offset;\n'
              '    final show = distance > 320;\n'
              '    if (show != _showScrollToBottom) {\n'
              '      setState(() => _showScrollToBottom = show);\n'
              '    }\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  @override\n'
              '  void dispose() {\n'
              '    PushService.instance.setOpenChat(null);\n'
              '    // Snapchat-style "After viewing": leaving the (whole) chat after reading it\n'
              '    // clears its messages from this device. Not from a thread — that would wipe\n'
              '    // the room from under a side conversation.\n'
              '    if (!_inThread) _store.expireViewed(_chatId);\n'
              '    if (RelayService.instance.openChatDigits ==\n'
              '        RelayService.digits(widget.chat.contact.phone)) {\n'
              '      RelayService.instance.openChatDigits = \'\';\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    _store.removeListener(_refreshSuggestions);\n'
              '    _store.removeListener(_maybeFollowNewMessage);\n'
              '    _store.removeListener(_maybeBuzzOnPoke);\n'
              '    ScreenshotWatch.instance.taken.removeListener(_onScreenshot);\n'
              '    ScreenshotWatch.instance.capturing.removeListener(_onCapturing);\n'
              '    RelayService.instance.screenshotPing.removeListener(_onRemoteScreenshot);\n'
              '    RelayService.instance.recordingPing.removeListener(_onRemoteRecording);\n'
              '    RelayService.instance.ghostShotPing.removeListener(_onRemoteGhostShot);\n'
              '    if (_isRealPeer(widget.chat.contact)) {\n'
              '      RelayService.instance.presencePing.removeListener(_onPresencePing);\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    if (RelayConfig.isEnabled) {\n'
              '      RelayService.instance.typingPing.removeListener(_onTypingPing);\n'
              '      _store.removeListener(_maybeSendReadReceipt);\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    _typingClear?.cancel();\n'
              '    _presenceSend?.cancel();\n'
              '    _presenceRevert?.cancel();\n'
              '    _jumpTimer?.cancel();\n'
              '    _highlightClear?.cancel();\n'
              '    WidgetsBinding.instance.removeObserver(this);\n'
              '    _scrollController.dispose();\n'
              '    _searchController.dispose();\n'
              '    super.dispose();\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  double _lastBottomInset = 0;\n'
              '\n'
              '  @override\n'
              '  void didChangeMetrics() {\n'
              '    // The keyboard opening or growing shrinks the transcript from the bottom.\n'
              '    // If you were reading the end (or just tapped the composer), follow it\n'
              '    // down so the latest message stays visible above the keyboard instead of\n'
              '    // hiding behind it. Only on an inset INCREASE and when already near the\n'
              '    // end, so someone scrolled up to read isn\'t yanked away.\n'
              '    final inset = WidgetsBinding\n'
              '            .instance.platformDispatcher.views.first.viewInsets.bottom;\n'
              '    final opened = inset > _lastBottomInset + 1;\n'
              '    _lastBottomInset = inset;\n'
              '    if (!opened || !_scrollController.hasClients) return;\n'
              '    final distance =\n'
              '        _scrollController.position.maxScrollExtent - _scrollController.offset;\n'
              '    if (distance < 400) {\n'
              '      WidgetsBinding.instance.addPostFrameCallback((_) {\n'
              '        if (mounted) _jumpToBottom(settleFrames: 6);\n'
              '      });\n'
              '    }\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  void _exitSearch() {\n'
              '    setState(() {\n'
              '      _searching = false;\n'
              '      _searchQuery = \'\';\n'
              '      _searchController.clear();\n'
              '    });\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  /// Closes search and scrolls to the tapped result in the full conversation,\n'
              '  /// flashing it — so you see the match in context.\n'
              '  void _exitSearchToMessage(String messageId) {\n'
              '    setState(() {\n'
              '      _searching = false;\n'
              '      _searchQuery = \'\';\n'
              '      _searchController.clear();\n'
              '    });\n'
              '    _jumpTimer?.cancel();\n'
              '    _jumpTimer = Timer(const Duration(milliseconds: 300),\n'
              '        () => _jumpToMessage(messageId));\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  /// Whether this screen is showing a thread rather than the room.\n'
              '  bool get _inThread => widget.threadRootId != null;\n'
              '\n'
              '  /// The transcript. In the room, everything that is not in a thread — a\n'
              '  /// thread\'s whole purpose is that its replies are not here. In a thread,\n'
              '  /// the message it hangs under followed by its replies.\n'
              '  List<Message> get _messages {\n'
              '    final all = _store.chatById(_chatId)?.messages ?? const <Message>[];\n'
              '    final root = widget.threadRootId;\n'
              '    if (root == null) {\n'
              '      return [for (final m in all) if (m.threadRootId == null) m];\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    return [\n'
              '      for (final m in all)\n'
              '        if (m.id == root || m.threadRootId == root) m\n'
              '    ];\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  /// Pins the view to the newest message, then keeps re-pinning for a few\n'
              '  /// frames while late-measuring bubbles (photos, GIFs, link previews)\n'
              '  /// grow the scroll extent under it. A single jump lands partway up the\n'
              '  /// transcript whenever anything below the fold was still measuring —\n'
              '  /// which is exactly "the chat doesn\'t open at the latest message".\n'
              '  void _jumpToBottom({int settleFrames = 12}) {\n'
              '    void step(int remaining) {\n'
              '      if (!mounted || !_scrollController.hasClients) return;\n'
              '      final position = _scrollController.position;\n'
              '      // The user grabbing the list ends the pinning — following a person\n'
              '      // who has started scrolling is fighting them.\n'
              '      if (position.isScrollingNotifier.value) return;\n'
              '      if (position.pixels < position.maxScrollExtent) {\n'
              '        _scrollController.jumpTo(position.maxScrollExtent);\n'
              '      }\n'
              '      if (remaining > 0) {\n'
              '        WidgetsBinding.instance\n'
              '            .addPostFrameCallback((_) => step(remaining - 1));\n'
              '      }\n'
              '    }\n'
              '\n'
              '    step(settleFrames);\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  Future<void> _animateToBottom() async {\n'
              '    if (!_scrollController.hasClients) return;\n'
              '    await _scrollController.animateTo(\n'
              '      _scrollController.position.maxScrollExtent,\n'
              '      duration: const Duration(milliseconds: 250),\n'
              '      curve: Curves.easeOut,\n'
              '    );\n'
              '    // One corrective settle: the extent can grow while the animation runs\n'
              '    // (an image below finished decoding), leaving the view just short.\n'
              '    if (mounted) _jumpToBottom(settleFrames: 4);\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  /// Replies the device suggested for the last message received.\n'
              '  List<String> _suggested = const [];\n'
              '\n'
              '  /// The message the current suggestions were generated for, so a rebuild\n'
              '  /// that changes nothing does not ask the model again.\n'
              '  String? _suggestedFor;\n'
              '\n'
              '  /// Asks the on-device model for replies to whatever just arrived.\n'
              '  ///\n'
              '  /// Cheap to call: it returns immediately on the phones that cannot do this\n'
              '  /// (most of them), when the last word was yours, and when the answer for\n'
              '  /// this message is already on screen.\n'
              '  Future<void> _refreshSuggestions() async {\n'
              '    final chat = _store.chatById(_chatId);\n'
              '    final messages = chat?.messages ?? const <Message>[];\n'
              '    if (!SmartReplies.worthSuggesting(messages)) {\n'
              '      if (_suggested.isNotEmpty && mounted) {\n'
              '        setState(() {\n'
              '          _suggested = const [];\n'
              '          _suggestedFor = null;\n'
              '        });\n'
              '      }\n'
              '      return;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    final latest = messages.last.id;\n'
              '    if (latest == _suggestedFor) return;\n'
              '    _suggestedFor = latest;\n'
              '    final replies = await SmartReplies.instance.suggest(\n'
              '      messages: messages,\n'
              '      contactName: widget.chat.contact.name,\n'
              '    );\n'
              '    if (!mounted) return;\n'
              '    // Another message may have landed while the model was thinking; that\n'
              '    // request\'s answer is the one to show.\n'
              '    if (_suggestedFor != latest) return;\n'
              '    setState(() => _suggested = replies);\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  /// First names that can be @mentioned in this chat — the group\'s members\n'
              '  /// (excluding you). Empty for one-to-one chats.\n'
              '  List<String> _mentionNames() {\n'
              '    if (!widget.chat.contact.isGroup) return const [];\n'
              '    final members = widget.chat.members.isNotEmpty\n'
              '        ? widget.chat.members\n'
              '        : MockData.contacts();\n'
              '    final me = AppState.profile.value.id;\n'
              '    return members\n'
              '        .where((u) => u.id != me && !u.isGroup)\n'
              '        .map((u) => u.name.split(\' \').first)\n'
              '        .toList();\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  /// Sends a poke — "hey" with no words. A real message underneath, so an\n'
              '  /// offline phone still gets it (mailbox), a pocket still buzzes (push)\n'
              '  /// and the chat list still counts it. The cooldown is the whole design:\n'
              '  /// a poke that can be spammed is a harassment button.\n'
              '  void _handlePoke() {\n'
              '    // One funnel (shared with the app-wide "Poke back" banner) mints the poke,\n'
              '    // checks the cooldown, and delivers it.\n'
              '    final wait = pokeChat(_chatId,\n'
              '        threadRootId: _inThread ? widget.threadRootId : null);\n'
              '    if (wait > 0) {\n'
              '      ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(SnackBar(\n'
              '          content: Text(\'You just poked them — give it ${wait}s.\')));\n'
              '      return;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    Haptics.press();\n'
              '    WidgetsBinding.instance.addPostFrameCallback((_) => _animateToBottom());\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  /// Buzzes when a poke lands while this conversation is on screen — the\n'
              '  /// buzz IS the feature; a silent poke is just a very short message.\n'
              '  /// Keyed by message id so a rebuild can\'t re-buzz the same poke.\n'
              '  String? _buzzedPokeId;\n'
              '  void _maybeBuzzOnPoke() {\n'
              '    final last = _store.chatById(_chatId)?.lastMessage;\n'
              '    if (last == null || !last.isPoke || last.isMe) return;\n'
              '    if (_buzzedPokeId == last.id) return;\n'
              '    _buzzedPokeId = last.id;\n'
              '    Haptics.press();\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  void _handleSend(String text) {\n'
              '    // No messaging until the recovery code exists (X\'s rule for encrypted\n'
              '    // DMs). The typed message rides through the gate: set the code up once\n'
              '    // and this very message sends, rather than being lost to the prompt.\n'
              '    if (!_isNoteToSelf && recoveryGateNeeded()) {\n'
              '      ensureRecoveryReady(context).then((ok) {\n'
              '        if (ok && mounted) _handleSend(text);\n'
              '      });\n'
              '      return;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    final now = DateTime.now();\n'
              '    _deliver(Message(\n'
              '      id: \'local_${now.microsecondsSinceEpoch}\',\n'
              '      text: text,\n'
              '      time: now,\n'
              '      isMe: true,\n'
              '      status: MessageStatus.sent,\n'
              '      replyTo: _replyTo,\n'
              '    ));\n'
              '    setState(() => _replyTo = null);\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  /// Schedules the current [text] to auto-send later. Returns true if set.\n'
              '  Future<bool> _scheduleMessage(String text) async {\n'
              '    final now = DateTime.now();\n'
              '    final date = await showDatePicker(\n'
              '      context: context,\n'
              '      initialDate: now,\n'
              '      firstDate: now,\n'
              '      lastDate: now.add(const Duration(days: 365)),\n'
              '      helpText: \'Send on\',\n'
              '    );\n'
              '    if (date == null || !mounted) return false;\n'
              '    final time = await showTimePicker(\n'
              '      context: context,\n'
              '      initialTime: TimeOfDay.fromDateTime(now.add(const Duration(hours: 1))),\n'
              '      helpText: \'Send at\',\n'
              '    );\n'
              '    if (time == null || !mounted) return false;\n'
              '    final when =\n'
              '        DateTime(date.year, date.month, date.day, time.hour, time.minute);\n'
              '    if (!when.isAfter(now)) {\n'
              '      ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(\n'
              '        const SnackBar(content: Text(\'Pick a time in the future\')),\n'
              '      );\n'
              '      return false;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    Scheduler.instance.schedule(\n'
              '      chatId: _chatId,\n'
              '      contactPhone: widget.chat.contact.phone,\n'
              '      text: text,\n'
              '      time: when,\n'
              '    );\n'
              '    ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(\n'
              '      SnackBar(\n'
              '          content: Text(\'Scheduled for ${DateFormatter.scheduleLabel(when)}\')),\n'
              '    );\n'
              '    return true;\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  void _showScheduledSheet() {\n'
              '    showModalBottomSheet<void>(\n'
              '      context: context,\n'
              '      builder: (sheetContext) => SafeArea(\n'
              '        child: ListenableBuilder(\n'
              '          listenable: Scheduler.instance,\n'
              '          builder: (context, _) {\n'
              '            final items = Scheduler.instance.pendingFor(_chatId);\n'
              '            if (items.isEmpty) {\n'
              '              Navigator.of(sheetContext).maybePop();\n'
              '              return const SizedBox.shrink();\n'
              '            }\n'
              '            return Column(\n'
              '              mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,\n'
              '              children: [\n'
              '                const Padding(\n'
              '                  padding: EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(16, 14, 16, 6),\n'
              '                  child: Align(\n'
              '                    alignment: Alignment.centerLeft,\n'
              '                    child: Text(\'Scheduled messages\',\n'
              '                        style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.w600)),\n'
              '                  ),\n'
              '                ),\n'
              '                for (final s in items)\n'
              '                  ListTile(\n'
              '                    leading: const Icon(Icons.schedule),\n'
              '                    title: Text(s.text,\n'
              '                        maxLines: 2, overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis),\n'
              '                    subtitle: Text(DateFormatter.scheduleLabel(s.time)),\n'
              '                    trailing: IconButton(\n'
              '                      icon: const Icon(Icons.close),\n'
              '                      tooltip: \'Cancel\',\n'
              '                      onPressed: () => Scheduler.instance.cancel(s.id),\n'
              '                    ),\n'
              '                  ),\n'
              '              ],\n'
              '            );\n'
              '          },\n'
              '        ),\n'
              '      ),\n'
              '    );\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  /// Picks a real photo from the device, shrinks it to fit the relay, and\n'
              '  /// sends it inline — device to device, no bucket in the middle.\n'
              '  Future<void> _handleSendImage({bool viewOnce = false}) async {\n'
              '    String? dataUri;\n'
              '    try {\n'
              '      dataUri = await PhotoPrep.pickPhoto();\n'
              '    } on FileRejected catch (e) {\n'
              '      if (mounted) {\n'
              '        ScaffoldMessenger.of(context)\n'
              '            .showSnackBar(SnackBar(content: Text(e.reason)));\n'
              '      }\n'
              '      return;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    if (dataUri == null) {\n'
              '      // Either the user cancelled (say nothing) or the image was unusable.\n'
              '      return;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    if (!mounted || !await _confirmRecipient()) return;\n'
              '    final now = DateTime.now();\n'
              '    _deliver(Message(\n'
              '      id: \'img_${now.microsecondsSinceEpoch}\',\n'
              '      text: \'\',\n'
              '      time: now,\n'
              '      isMe: true,\n'
              '      status: MessageStatus.sent,\n'
              '      isImage: true,\n'
              '      imageUrl: dataUri,\n'
              '      imageSeed: now.microsecondsSinceEpoch % 6,\n'
              '      viewOnce: viewOnce,\n'
              '      replyTo: _replyTo,\n'
              '    ));\n'
              '    setState(() => _replyTo = null);\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  /// Sends a GIF picked from the composer. It rides as an image message with\n'
              '  /// a real URL, so it animates in the bubble and travels over the relay like\n'
              '  /// any other photo — the file itself stays on the GIF provider\'s CDN.\n'
              '  Future<void> _handleSendGif(String url) async {\n'
              '    if (!await _confirmRecipient()) return;\n'
              '    final now = DateTime.now();\n'
              '    _deliver(Message(\n'
              '      id: \'gif_${now.microsecondsSinceEpoch}\',\n'
              '      text: \'\',\n'
              '      time: now,\n'
              '      isMe: true,\n'
              '      status: MessageStatus.sent,\n'
              '      isImage: true,\n'
              '      imageUrl: url,\n'
              '      imageSeed: now.microsecondsSinceEpoch % 6,\n'
              '      replyTo: _replyTo,\n'
              '    ));\n'
              '    setState(() => _replyTo = null);\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  void _handleSendVoice(int seconds,\n'
              '      {String? audioUrl, String? audioPath, String? audioKey}) {\n'
              '    // No audio, no message: the input bar only calls back with nothing when\n'
              '    // capture or upload failed, and it has already said so.\n'
              '    if (audioUrl == null && audioPath == null) return;\n'
              '    final now = DateTime.now();\n'
              '    _deliver(Message(\n'
              '      id: \'voice_${now.microsecondsSinceEpoch}\',\n'
              '      text: \'\',\n'
              '      time: now,\n'
              '      isMe: true,\n'
              '      status: MessageStatus.sent,\n'
              '      isVoice: true,\n'
              '      voiceSeconds: seconds,\n'
              '      audioUrl: audioUrl,\n'
              '      audioPath: audioPath,\n'
              '      audioKey: audioKey,\n'
              '    ));\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  /// Picks a file and sends it peer-to-peer over a WebRTC data channel (the\n'
              '  /// bytes never touch a server). Requires a real, online contact.\n'
              '  Future<void> _handleSendDocument() async {\n'
              '    if (!RelayConfig.isEnabled || !_isRealPeer(widget.chat.contact)) {\n'
              '      _showComingSoon(context, \'Direct file sending (needs a real contact)\');\n'
              '      return;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    if (!await _confirmRecipient()) return;\n'
              '    final result = await FilePicker.pickFiles(withData: true);\n'
              '    if (result == null || result.files.isEmpty || !mounted) return;\n'
              '    final f = result.files.first;\n'
              '    final bytes = f.bytes;\n'
              '    if (bytes == null) {\n'
              '      ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(\n'
              '        const SnackBar(content: Text(\'Couldn\\\'t read that file\')),\n'
              '      );\n'
              '      return;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    // Moderate before anything leaves the device: executables, scripts, and\n'
              '    // blocked content are refused with a reason.\n'
              '    final verdict = FileModeration.inspectFile(Uint8List.fromList(bytes));\n'
              '    if (!verdict.allowed) {\n'
              '      ScaffoldMessenger.of(context)\n'
              '          .showSnackBar(SnackBar(content: Text(verdict.reason!)));\n'
              '      return;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    // Record a local marker in the chat, then stream the bytes directly.\n'
              '    _deliver(Message(\n'
              '      id: \'file_${DateTime.now().microsecondsSinceEpoch}\',\n'
              '      text: \'📎 ${f.name}\',\n'
              '      time: DateTime.now(),\n'
              '      isMe: true,\n'
              '      status: MessageStatus.sent,\n'
              '    ));\n'
              '    FileTransfer.instance.sendFile(\n'
              '      widget.chat.contact.phone,\n'
              '      widget.chat.contact.name,\n'
              '      f.name,\n'
              '      Uint8List.fromList(bytes),\n'
              '    );\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  Future<void> _handleSendLocation() async {\n'
              '    // A dedicated share-location picker, NOT the full Maps map: your current\n'
              '    // location up top, a search box, saved places, and a way onto the map to\n'
              '    // drop a pin only when the place has no address. Sharing where you are is\n'
              '    // a list, not an exploration.\n'
              '    final picked = await Navigator.of(context).push<GeoResult>(\n'
              '      MaterialPageRoute(builder: (_) => const ShareLocationScreen()),\n'
              '    );\n'
              '    if (picked == null || !mounted) return;\n'
              '    if (!await _confirmRecipient()) return;\n'
              '    final now = DateTime.now();\n'
              '    final label = picked.name.trim();\n'
              '    _deliver(Message(\n'
              '      id: \'loc_${now.microsecondsSinceEpoch}\',\n'
              '      // The place\'s own name, not "Shared location". A dropped pin is reverse\n'
              '      // geocoded on the way through, so even a point in a field arrives as a\n'
              '      // street rather than as two numbers.\n'
              '      text: label.isEmpty ? \'Shared location\' : label,\n'
              '      time: now,\n'
              '      isMe: true,\n'
              '      status: MessageStatus.sent,\n'
              '      isLocation: true,\n'
              '      locationLat: picked.lat,\n'
              '      locationLng: picked.lng,\n'
              '      locationLabel: label.isEmpty ? \'Shared location\' : label,\n'
              '    ));\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  /// Opens a picker of the people you chat with, and shares the chosen one as\n'
              '  /// a contact card.\n'
              '  void _pickContactToShare() {\n'
              '    // Everyone you could plausibly share: 1:1 chats and members of your\n'
              '    // groups (people you know but may never have messaged directly), minus\n'
              '    // yourself and this chat\'s own peer.\n'
              '    final me = AppState.profile.value.id;\n'
              '    final seen = <String>{};\n'
              '    final contacts = <AppUser>[];\n'
              '    void consider(AppUser u) {\n'
              '      if (u.isGroup || u.phone.isEmpty) return;\n'
              '      if (u.id == me || u.id == widget.chat.contact.id) return;\n'
              '      if (seen.add(RelayService.digits(u.phone))) contacts.add(u);\n'
              '    }\n'
              '\n'
              '    for (final chat in _store.allChats) {\n'
              '      consider(chat.contact);\n'
              '      chat.members.forEach(consider);\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    contacts.sort(\n'
              '        (a, b) => a.name.toLowerCase().compareTo(b.name.toLowerCase()));\n'
              '    showModalBottomSheet<void>(\n'
              '      context: context,\n'
              '      builder: (sheetContext) => SafeArea(\n'
              '        child: Column(\n'
              '          mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,\n'
              '          children: [\n'
              '            const Padding(\n'
              '              padding: EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(20, 16, 20, 6),\n'
              '              child: Align(\n'
              '                alignment: Alignment.centerLeft,\n'
              '                child: Text(\'Share contact\',\n'
              '                    style:\n'
              '                        TextStyle(fontSize: 16, fontWeight: FontWeight.w600)),\n'
              '              ),\n'
              '            ),\n'
              '            if (contacts.isEmpty)\n'
              '              Padding(\n'
              '                padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(24, 20, 24, 32),\n'
              '                child: Text(\n'
              '                  \'No one to share yet — contacts appear here once you have \'\n'
              '                  \'other chats or groups.\',\n'
              '                  textAlign: TextAlign.center,\n'
              '                  style: TextStyle(color: AppColors.subtle(context)),\n'
              '                ),\n'
              '              )\n'
              '            else\n'
              '              Flexible(\n'
              '                child: ListView(\n'
              '                  shrinkWrap: true,\n'
              '                  children: [\n'
              '                    for (final c in contacts)\n'
              '                      ListTile(\n'
              '                        leading: UserAvatar(user: c, radius: 20),\n'
              '                        title: Text(c.name),\n'
              '                        subtitle: c.phone.isNotEmpty ? Text(c.phone) : null,\n'
              '                        onTap: () {\n'
              '                          Navigator.of(sheetContext).pop();\n'
              '                          _sendContactCard(c);\n'
              '                        },\n'
              '                      ),\n'
              '                  ],\n'
              '                ),\n'
              '              ),\n'
              '          ],\n'
              '        ),\n'
              '      ),\n'
              '    );\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  Future<void> _sendContactCard(AppUser contact) async {\n'
              '    if (!await _confirmRecipient()) return;\n'
              '    final now = DateTime.now();\n'
              '    _deliver(Message(\n'
              '      id: \'contact_${now.microsecondsSinceEpoch}\',\n'
              '      text: \'Contact: ${contact.name}\',\n'
              '      time: now,\n'
              '      isMe: true,\n'
              '      status: MessageStatus.sent,\n'
              '      isContact: true,\n'
              '      contactName: contact.name,\n'
              '      contactPhone: contact.phone,\n'
              '    ));\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  /// Opens a shared-location message on a full-screen, interactive\n'
              '  /// OpenStreetMap; from there the user can hand off to their maps app.\n'
              '  void _openLocation(Message m) {\n'
              '    Navigator.of(context).push(\n'
              '      MaterialPageRoute(\n'
              '        builder: (_) => LocationMapScreen(\n'
              '          lat: m.locationLat ?? 0,\n'
              '          lng: m.locationLng ?? 0,\n'
              '          label: m.locationLabel ?? \'\',\n'
              '        ),\n'
              '      ),\n'
              '    );\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  /// Opens (or starts) a chat with a shared contact card\'s person.\n'
              '  void _openSharedContact(Message m) {\n'
              '    final phone = m.contactPhone ?? \'\';\n'
              '    final name = m.contactName ?? \'Contact\';\n'
              '    var chat = phone.isEmpty ? null : _store.chatWithContact(phone);\n'
              '    if (chat == null) {\n'
              '      final user = AppUser(\n'
              '        id: phone.isEmpty ? name : phone,\n'
              '        name: name,\n'
              '        avatarColor: \'#7A5CFF\',\n'
              '        about: \'Available\',\n'
              '        phone: phone,\n'
              '      );\n'
              '      chat = Chat(id: \'chat_${user.id}\', contact: user, messages: const []);\n'
              '      _store.upsert(chat);\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    Navigator.of(context).push(\n'
              '      MaterialPageRoute(builder: (_) => ChatScreen(chat: chat!)),\n'
              '    );\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  /// Stores an outgoing [message] and either delivers it over the relay (to a\n'
              '  /// real number-based peer) or triggers a simulated reply (demo contact).\n'
              '  /// When this chat has the "confirm before sending" safeguard on, asks the\n'
              '  /// user to confirm the recipient before anything is sent. Returns true when\n'
              '  /// it\'s safe to proceed (either off, or the user confirmed).\n'
              '  Future<bool> _confirmRecipient() async {\n'
              '    final chat = _store.chatById(_chatId);\n'
              '    if (chat == null || !chat.confirmBeforeSend) return true;\n'
              '    final contact = chat.contact;\n'
              '    final ok = await showDialog<bool>(\n'
              '      context: context,\n'
              '      builder: (dialogContext) => Dialog(\n'
              '        shape:\n'
              '            RoundedRectangleBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(24)),\n'
              '        child: Padding(\n'
              '          padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(24, 26, 24, 18),\n'
              '          child: Column(\n'
              '            mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,\n'
              '            crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,\n'
              '            children: [\n'
              '              Center(child: UserAvatar(user: contact, radius: 30)),\n'
              '              const SizedBox(height: 12),\n'
              '              const Text(\n'
              '                \'Send to the right chat?\',\n'
              '                textAlign: TextAlign.center,\n'
              '                style:\n'
              '                    TextStyle(fontSize: 18, fontWeight: FontWeight.w700),\n'
              '              ),\n'
              '              const SizedBox(height: 8),\n'
              '              Text(\n'
              '                contact.isGroup\n'
              '                    ? \'This message will go to everyone in "${contact.name}".\'\n'
              '                    : \'This message will be sent to ${contact.name}.\',\n'
              '                textAlign: TextAlign.center,\n'
              '                style: TextStyle(\n'
              '                    fontSize: 14, height: 1.4, color: AppColors.subtle(context)),\n'
              '              ),\n'
              '              const SizedBox(height: 18),\n'
              '              FilledButton(\n'
              '                style: FilledButton.styleFrom(\n'
              '                  backgroundColor: AppColors.accentOn(dialogContext),\n'
              '                  foregroundColor: AppColors.onAccent(dialogContext),\n'
              '                  padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 14),\n'
              '                  shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(\n'
              '                      borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(26)),\n'
              '                ),\n'
              '                onPressed: () => Navigator.of(dialogContext).pop(true),\n'
              '                child: const Text(\'Send\'),\n'
              '              ),\n'
              '              TextButton(\n'
              '                onPressed: () => Navigator.of(dialogContext).pop(false),\n'
              '                child: Text(\'Cancel\',\n'
              '                    style: TextStyle(color: AppColors.subtle(context))),\n'
              '              ),\n'
              '            ],\n'
              '          ),\n'
              '        ),\n'
              '      ),\n'
              '    );\n'
              '    return ok ?? false;\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  /// Whether [message] may leave this conversation by forward or copy.\n'
              '  ///\n'
              '  /// Two ways it may not: this chat is protected, or the message arrived\n'
              '  /// carrying the sender\'s own protection. The second is the one that\n'
              '  /// matters — turning your own setting off must not unlock what somebody\n'
              '  /// else sent you under theirs.\n'
              '  bool _mayLeaveChat(Message message) =>\n'
              '      !_store.isProtected(_chatId) && !message.protected;\n'
              '\n'
              '  /// The same question for a selection, which is only exportable if every\n'
              '  /// message in it is.\n'
              '  bool get _selectionMayLeave => _selectedMessages.every(_mayLeaveChat);\n'
              '\n'
              '  /// Watches for a screenshot while this conversation is on screen, and only\n'
              '  /// when it is protected.\n'
              '  ///\n'
              '  /// Attributed to whatever was actually being looked at: the notifier fires\n'
              '  /// app-wide, so a screen that is not on top must ignore it or a screenshot\n'
              '  /// of the chat list would be announced as a screenshot of a conversation.\n'
              '  void _onScreenshot() {\n'
              '    if (!mounted || !ModalRoute.of(context)!.isCurrent) return;\n'
              '    if (!_store.isProtected(_chatId)) return;\n'
              '    _store.noteScreenshot(_chatId, byMe: true);\n'
              '    if (RelayConfig.isEnabled && _isRealPeer(widget.chat.contact)) {\n'
              '      RelayService.instance.sendScreenshotNotice(widget.chat.contact.phone);\n'
              '      // The Snapchat promise, kept even for a closed app: being told about\n'
              '      // a screenshot only when you happen to be looking is not being told.\n'
              '      final myName = AppState.profile.value.name;\n'
              '      PushService.instance.notify(widget.chat.contact.phone,\n'
              '          title: myName.isEmpty ? \'Screenshot\' : myName,\n'
              '          body: \'Took a screenshot!\');\n'
              '    }\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  /// What sits where the conversation was while the screen is being captured.\n'
              '  Widget _capturedNotice(BuildContext context) => Center(\n'
              '        child: Padding(\n'
              '          padding: const EdgeInsets.all(32),\n'
              '          child: Column(\n'
              '            mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,\n'
              '            children: [\n'
              '              Icon(Icons.screenshot_monitor_outlined,\n'
              '                  size: 54, color: AppColors.subtle(context)),\n'
              '              const SizedBox(height: 18),\n'
              '              const Text(\'Hidden while the screen is being captured\',\n'
              '                  textAlign: TextAlign.center,\n'
              '                  style:\n'
              '                      TextStyle(fontSize: 18, fontWeight: FontWeight.w700)),\n'
              '              const SizedBox(height: 10),\n'
              '              Text(\n'
              '                \'This chat is protected, and the screen is being recorded or \'\n'
              '                \'mirrored. The conversation comes back on its own when that \'\n'
              '                \'stops. The other person has been told.\',\n'
              '                textAlign: TextAlign.center,\n'
              '                style: TextStyle(\n'
              '                    fontSize: 14.5,\n'
              '                    height: 1.45,\n'
              '                    color: AppColors.subtle(context)),\n'
              '              ),\n'
              '            ],\n'
              '          ),\n'
              '        ),\n'
              '      );\n'
              '\n'
              '  /// A recording started or stopped. Announced ONCE per recording, on the\n'
              '  /// edge rather than on every rebuild — a notice per frame would bury the\n'
              '  /// conversation it is warning about.\n'
              '  void _onCapturing() {\n'
              '    if (!mounted) return;\n'
              '    final on = ScreenshotWatch.instance.capturing.value;\n'
              '    setState(() {});\n'
              '    if (!on || !_store.isProtected(_chatId)) return;\n'
              '    if (!ModalRoute.of(context)!.isCurrent) return;\n'
              '    _store.noteScreenshot(_chatId, byMe: true, recording: true);\n'
              '    if (RelayConfig.isEnabled && _isRealPeer(widget.chat.contact)) {\n'
              '      RelayService.instance.sendRecordingNotice(widget.chat.contact.phone);\n'
              '    }\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  /// The far end is recording this conversation.\n'
              '  void _onRemoteRecording() {\n'
              '    if (!mounted) return;\n'
              '    final from = RelayService.instance.recordingFromDigits;\n'
              '    if (from.isEmpty ||\n'
              '        from != RelayService.digits(widget.chat.contact.phone)) {\n'
              '      return;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    _store.noteScreenshot(_chatId, byMe: false, recording: true);\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  /// The far end screenshotted this conversation.\n'
              '  void _onRemoteScreenshot() {\n'
              '    if (!mounted) return;\n'
              '    final from = RelayService.instance.screenshotFromDigits;\n'
              '    if (from.isEmpty ||\n'
              '        from != RelayService.digits(widget.chat.contact.phone)) {\n'
              '      return;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    _store.noteScreenshot(_chatId, byMe: false);\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  /// The far end screenshotted a ghost message from this conversation while\n'
              '  /// it was open on their screen.\n'
              '  void _onRemoteGhostShot() {\n'
              '    if (!mounted) return;\n'
              '    final from = RelayService.instance.ghostShotFromDigits;\n'
              '    if (from.isEmpty ||\n'
              '        from != RelayService.digits(widget.chat.contact.phone)) {\n'
              '      return;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    _store.noteScreenshot(_chatId, byMe: false, ghost: true);\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  void _deliver(Message rawMessage) {\n'
              '    // Stamped here rather than at each of the eight places a message is\n'
              '    // built, so a send path added later carries the flag without anybody\n'
              '    // remembering to add it. The setting belongs to whoever wrote the words,\n'
              '    // which is why it is read from this chat and then travels.\n'
              '    var message = rawMessage;\n'
              '    if (_store.isProtected(_chatId)) {\n'
              '      message = message.copyWith(protected: true);\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    // Same funnel again: a marketplace chat\'s messages say so on the wire,\n'
              '    // which is the only way the OTHER side\'s brand-new conversation knows\n'
              '    // to file itself in the marketplace section.\n'
              '    if (_store.chatById(_chatId)?.marketplace ?? false) {\n'
              '      message = message.copyWith(marketplace: true);\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    // Same funnel, same reason: every send path lands here, so a thread reply\n'
              '    // cannot escape into the room because one of them forgot.\n'
              '    if (_inThread) {\n'
              '      message = message.copyWith(threadRootId: widget.threadRootId);\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    _store.addMessage(_chatId, message);\n'
              '    WidgetsBinding.instance.addPostFrameCallback((_) => _animateToBottom());\n'
              '    if (!RelayConfig.isEnabled) return;\n'
              '    if (widget.chat.contact.isGroup) {\n'
              '      // Groups have no server side: the message is fanned out to each member\'s\n'
              '      // inbox, encrypted separately for every one of them.\n'
              '      final chat = _store.chatById(_chatId);\n'
              '      if (chat != null) RelayService.instance.sendToGroup(chat, message);\n'
              '    } else if (_isRealPeer(widget.chat.contact)) {\n'
              '      RelayService.instance.send(widget.chat.contact.phone, message);\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    // No simulated replies: only real people answer here.\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  /// A real, number-identified peer (chat started with an actual phone number),\n'
              '  /// as opposed to a seeded demo contact or a group.\n'
              '  bool _isRealPeer(AppUser c) =>\n'
              '      !c.isGroup && c.phone.isNotEmpty && c.id == c.phone;\n'
              '\n'
              '  /// Every inbox an in-chat event (edit, reaction, receipt, …) goes to: the\n'
              '  /// peer for a real 1:1 chat, every member for a group, nobody for demo\n'
              '  /// chats or note-to-self. Events fan out exactly like the messages they\n'
              '  /// are about.\n'
              '  List<String> _relayPhones() {\n'
              '    // No RelayConfig gate here: every RelayService send is already a no-op\n'
              '    // without a configured relay, and gating the *decision* on it hid the\n'
              '    // delete-for-everyone option in local/dev builds.\n'
              '    final c = widget.chat.contact;\n'
              '    if (c.isGroup) {\n'
              '      final chat = _store.chatById(_chatId);\n'
              '      return chat == null ? const [] : RelayService.groupRecipients(chat);\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    return _isRealPeer(c) ? [c.phone] : const [];\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  void _startReply(Message message) {\n'
              '    setState(() {\n'
              '      _replyTo = ReplyInfo(\n'
              '        senderName: widget.chat.contact.name,\n'
              '        text: message.isImage\n'
              '            ? \'📷 Photo\'\n'
              '            : message.isVoice\n'
              '                ? \'🎤 Voice message\'\n'
              '                : message.text,\n'
              '        isMe: message.isMe,\n'
              '        messageId: message.id,\n'
              '      );\n'
              '    });\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  void _enterSelection(String id) => setState(() => _selectedIds\n'
              '    ..clear()\n'
              '    ..add(id));\n'
              '\n'
              '  void _toggleSelect(String id) {\n'
              '    setState(() {\n'
              '      if (!_selectedIds.remove(id)) _selectedIds.add(id);\n'
              '    });\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  void _exitSelection() => setState(_selectedIds.clear);\n'
              '\n'
              '  List<Message> get _selectedMessages =>\n'
              '      _messages.where((m) => _selectedIds.contains(m.id)).toList();\n'
              '\n'
              '  Future<void> _deleteSelected() async {\n'
              '    final selected = _selectedMessages;\n'
              '    final allMine = selected.isNotEmpty && selected.every((m) => m.isMe);\n'
              '    if (allMine && _relayPhones().isNotEmpty) {\n'
              '      final forEveryone = await showModalBottomSheet<bool>(\n'
              '        context: context,\n'
              '        builder: (sheetContext) => SafeArea(\n'
              '          child: Column(\n'
              '            mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,\n'
              '            children: [\n'
              '              ListTile(\n'
              '                leading: const Icon(Icons.delete_outline),\n'
              '                title: const Text(\'Delete for me\'),\n'
              '                onTap: () => Navigator.of(sheetContext).pop(false),\n'
              '              ),\n'
              '              ListTile(\n'
              '                leading: const Icon(Icons.delete_forever, color: Colors.red),\n'
              '                title: const Text(\'Delete for everyone\',\n'
              '                    style: TextStyle(color: Colors.red)),\n'
              '                onTap: () => Navigator.of(sheetContext).pop(true),\n'
              '              ),\n'
              '            ],\n'
              '          ),\n'
              '        ),\n'
              '      );\n'
              '      if (forEveryone == null) return; // dismissed — keep the selection\n'
              '      for (final m in selected) {\n'
              '        _store.deleteMessage(_chatId, m.id, forEveryone: forEveryone);\n'
              '        if (forEveryone) {\n'
              '          for (final phone in _relayPhones()) {\n'
              '            RelayService.instance.sendDelete(phone, m.id);\n'
              '          }\n'
              '        }\n'
              '      }\n'
              '      _exitSelection();\n'
              '      return;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    for (final id in _selectedIds) {\n'
              '      _store.deleteMessage(_chatId, id);\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    _exitSelection();\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  void _starSelected() {\n'
              '    for (final id in _selectedIds) {\n'
              '      if (!_store.isStarred(_chatId, id)) _store.toggleStar(_chatId, id);\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    _exitSelection();\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  void _copySelected() {\n'
              '    final text = _selectedMessages.map((m) => m.text).join(\'\\n\');\n'
              '    Clipboard.setData(ClipboardData(text: text));\n'
              '    _exitSelection();\n'
              '    ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(\n'
              '      const SnackBar(content: Text(\'Messages copied\')),\n'
              '    );\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  void _forwardSelected() {\n'
              '    final text = _selectedMessages.map((m) => m.text).join(\'\\n\');\n'
              '    _exitSelection();\n'
              '    Navigator.of(context).push(\n'
              '      MaterialPageRoute(builder: (_) => ForwardScreen(text: text)),\n'
              '    );\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  List<Message> get _visibleMessages {\n'
              '    final q = _searchQuery.trim().toLowerCase();\n'
              '    if (!_searching || q.isEmpty) return _messages;\n'
              '    return _messages.where((m) => m.text.toLowerCase().contains(q)).toList();\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  List<Widget> _buildItems() {\n'
              '    final items = <Widget>[];\n'
              '    DateTime? lastDay;\n'
              '    for (final m in _visibleMessages) {\n'
              '      final day = DateTime(m.time.year, m.time.month, m.time.day);\n'
              '      if (lastDay == null || day != lastDay) {\n'
              '        items.add(_DayHeader(label: DateFormatter.messageDayHeader(m.time)));\n'
              '        lastDay = day;\n'
              '      }\n'
              '      if (!_searching && m.id == _unreadAnchorId) {\n'
              '        items.add(_UnreadDivider(count: _initialUnread));\n'
              '      }\n'
              '      final bubble = MessageBubble(\n'
              '        message: m,\n'
              '        starred: _store.isStarred(_chatId, m.id),\n'
              '        onLongPress: _selectionMode ? null : () => _showMessageActions(m),\n'
              '        onTap: _searching\n'
              '            ? () => _exitSearchToMessage(m.id)\n'
              '            : (_canOpenImage(m) && !_selectionMode\n'
              '                ? () => _openImage(m)\n'
              '                : (_canOpenGhost(m) && !_selectionMode\n'
              '                    ? () => _openGhost(m)\n'
              '                    : (_canAnswerRequest(m) && !_selectionMode\n'
              '                        ? () => _answerPaymentRequest(m)\n'
              '                        : null))),\n'
              '        onDoubleTapDown:\n'
              '            _selectionMode ? null : (d) => _lastDoubleTapPos = d.globalPosition,\n'
              '        onDoubleTap: _selectionMode ? null : () => _quickReact(m),\n'
              '        onReplyTap: m.replyTo?.messageId == null\n'
              '            ? null\n'
              '            : () => _jumpToMessage(m.replyTo!.messageId!),\n'
              '        onOpenLocation:\n'
              '            m.isLocation && !_selectionMode ? () => _openLocation(m) : null,\n'
              '        onOpenContact:\n'
              '            m.isContact && !_selectionMode ? () => _openSharedContact(m) : null,\n'
              '        onPollVote:\n'
              '            m.isPoll && !_selectionMode ? (i) => _handleVotePoll(m, i) : null,\n'
              '        // Pay your share, when the bill has one for you that isn\'t paid yet.\n'
              '        onPayBillShare: m.isBillSplit && !_selectionMode && !m.isMe\n'
              '            ? () => _payBillShare(m)\n'
              '            : null,\n'
              '        // The sender reads what came back; everybody else fills it in.\n'
              '        onOpenForm: m.isForm && !_selectionMode\n'
              '            ? () => m.isMe ? _openFormResponses(m) : _handleFillForm(m)\n'
              '            : null,\n'
              '        // A call record is the natural place to return the call from.\n'
              '        onCallBack: m.isCallEvent &&\n'
              '                !_selectionMode &&\n'
              '                !widget.chat.contact.isGroup\n'
              '            ? () => CallService.instance\n'
              '                .startOutgoing(widget.chat.contact, video: m.callVideo)\n'
              '            : null,\n'
              '        // And a poke is the natural place to poke back from.\n'
              '        onPokeBack: m.isPoke && !m.isMe && !_selectionMode\n'
              '            ? _handlePoke\n'
              '            : null,\n'
              '      );\n'
              '\n'
              '      final key = _messageKeys.putIfAbsent(m.id, () => GlobalKey());\n'
              '      final highlighted = _highlightedId == m.id;\n'
              '      final keyed = Container(\n'
              '        key: key,\n'
              '        color: highlighted\n'
              '            ? AppColors.accentOn(context).withValues(alpha: 0.18)\n'
              '            : null,\n'
              '        child: bubble,\n'
              '      );\n'
              '\n'
              '      final Widget row;\n'
              '      if (_selectionMode) {\n'
              '        final selected = _selectedIds.contains(m.id);\n'
              '        row = GestureDetector(\n'
              '          behavior: HitTestBehavior.opaque,\n'
              '          onTap: () => _toggleSelect(m.id),\n'
              '          onLongPress: () => _toggleSelect(m.id),\n'
              '          child: Container(\n'
              '            color: selected\n'
              '                ? AppColors.accentOn(context).withValues(alpha: 0.16)\n'
              '                : null,\n'
              '            child: keyed,\n'
              '          ),\n'
              '        );\n'
              '      } else {\n'
              '        row = Dismissible(\n'
              '          key: ValueKey(\'msg_${m.id}\'),\n'
              '          direction: DismissDirection.startToEnd,\n'
              '          dismissThresholds: const {DismissDirection.startToEnd: 0.25},\n'
              '          confirmDismiss: (_) async {\n'
              '            _startReply(m);\n'
              '            return false; // snap back; we only use the swipe to trigger reply\n'
              '          },\n'
              '          background: const Padding(\n'
              '            padding: EdgeInsets.only(left: 24),\n'
              '            child: Align(\n'
              '              alignment: Alignment.centerLeft,\n'
              '              child: Icon(Icons.reply, color: Colors.grey),\n'
              '            ),\n'
              '          ),\n'
              '          child: keyed,\n'
              '        );\n'
              '      }\n'
              '      // Keyed by message id so each message animates in exactly once and\n'
              '      // never re-animates on later rebuilds (reactions, status, etc.).\n'
              '      items.add(_MessageEntrance(key: ValueKey(\'anim_${m.id}\'), child: row));\n'
              '\n'
              '      // The thread hanging under this message, if any. Only in the room —\n'
              '      // inside a thread the root is the thing you are already reading, and a\n'
              '      // line offering to open it would lead back to here.\n'
              '      if (!_inThread && !_selectionMode) {\n'
              '        final replies = _store.threadReplyCount(_chatId, m.id);\n'
              '        if (replies > 0) {\n'
              '          items.add(_ThreadLine(\n'
              '            count: replies,\n'
              '            isMe: m.isMe,\n'
              '            onTap: () => _openThread(m),\n'
              '          ));\n'
              '        }\n'
              '      }\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    return items;\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  /// Opens the thread hanging under [message] — the same screen, showing the\n'
              '  /// root and its replies instead of the room.\n'
              '  void _openThread(Message message) {\n'
              '    Navigator.of(context).push(MaterialPageRoute(\n'
              '      builder: (_) => ChatScreen(chat: widget.chat, threadRootId: message.id),\n'
              '    ));\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  /// Scrolls to the original [messageId] (the quoted message) and flashes it.\n'
              '  void _jumpToMessage(String messageId) {\n'
              '    final key = _messageKeys[messageId];\n'
              '    final ctx = key?.currentContext;\n'
              '    if (ctx == null) return;\n'
              '    Scrollable.ensureVisible(\n'
              '      ctx,\n'
              '      duration: const Duration(milliseconds: 300),\n'
              '      curve: Curves.easeInOut,\n'
              '      alignment: 0.3,\n'
              '    );\n'
              '    setState(() => _highlightedId = messageId);\n'
              '    _highlightClear?.cancel();\n'
              '    _highlightClear = Timer(const Duration(milliseconds: 1200), () {\n'
              '      if (mounted && _highlightedId == messageId) {\n'
              '        setState(() => _highlightedId = null);\n'
              '      }\n'
              '    });\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  /// Pins [message]. Pinning is unlimited and free for everyone.\n'
              '  void _tryPin(Message message) {\n'
              '    _store.pinMessage(_chatId, message.id);\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  /// A bottom sheet listing every pinned message, each with jump + unpin.\n'
              '  void _showPinnedSheet() {\n'
              '    showModalBottomSheet<void>(\n'
              '      context: context,\n'
              '      showDragHandle: true,\n'
              '      builder: (sheetContext) => ListenableBuilder(\n'
              '        listenable: _store,\n'
              '        builder: (context, _) {\n'
              '          final chat = _store.chatById(_chatId);\n'
              '          final ids = chat?.pinnedMessageIds ?? const <String>[];\n'
              '          final msgs = <Message>[];\n'
              '          for (final id in ids.reversed) {\n'
              '            final match = chat!.messages.where((m) => m.id == id);\n'
              '            if (match.isNotEmpty) msgs.add(match.first);\n'
              '          }\n'
              '          if (msgs.isEmpty) {\n'
              '            // Nothing left pinned — close the sheet.\n'
              '            WidgetsBinding.instance.addPostFrameCallback((_) {\n'
              '              if (Navigator.of(sheetContext).canPop()) {\n'
              '                Navigator.of(sheetContext).pop();\n'
              '              }\n'
              '            });\n'
              '            return const SizedBox.shrink();\n'
              '          }\n'
              '          return SafeArea(\n'
              '            child: Column(\n'
              '              mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,\n'
              '              children: [\n'
              '                Padding(\n'
              '                  padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(20, 0, 20, 8),\n'
              '                  child: Row(\n'
              '                    children: [\n'
              '                      const Icon(Icons.push_pin, size: 18),\n'
              '                      const SizedBox(width: 8),\n'
              '                      Text(\'${msgs.length} pinned\',\n'
              '                          style: const TextStyle(\n'
              '                              fontSize: 16, fontWeight: FontWeight.w600)),\n'
              '                      const Spacer(),\n'
              '                      TextButton(\n'
              '                        onPressed: () {\n'
              '                          _store.unpinAll(_chatId);\n'
              '                          Navigator.of(sheetContext).pop();\n'
              '                        },\n'
              '                        child: const Text(\'Unpin all\'),\n'
              '                      ),\n'
              '                    ],\n'
              '                  ),\n'
              '                ),\n'
              '                Flexible(\n'
              '                  child: ListView.builder(\n'
              '                    shrinkWrap: true,\n'
              '                    itemCount: msgs.length,\n'
              '                    itemBuilder: (context, i) {\n'
