              '                      final m = msgs[i];\n'
              '                      return ListTile(\n'
              '                        leading: const Icon(Icons.push_pin_outlined),\n'
              '                        title: Text(\n'
              '                          m.isVoice\n'
              '                              ? \'Voice message\'\n'
              '                              : m.isImage\n'
              '                                  ? \'Photo\'\n'
              '                                  : m.text,\n'
              '                          maxLines: 2,\n'
              '                          overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,\n'
              '                        ),\n'
              '                        trailing: IconButton(\n'
              '                          icon: const Icon(Icons.close),\n'
              '                          tooltip: \'Unpin\',\n'
              '                          onPressed: () => _store.unpinMessage(_chatId, m.id),\n'
              '                        ),\n'
              '                        onTap: () {\n'
              '                          Navigator.of(sheetContext).pop();\n'
              '                          _jumpToMessage(m.id);\n'
              '                        },\n'
              '                      );\n'
              '                    },\n'
              '                  ),\n'
              '                ),\n'
              '              ],\n'
              '            ),\n'
              '          );\n'
              '        },\n'
              '      ),\n'
              '    );\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  void _showMessageActions(Message message) {\n'
              '    Haptics.press();\n'
              '    // A deleted tombstone only offers removal from this device.\n'
              '    if (message.isDeleted) {\n'
              '      showModalBottomSheet<void>(\n'
              '        context: context,\n'
              '        builder: (sheetContext) => SafeArea(\n'
              '          child: ListTile(\n'
              '            leading: const Icon(Icons.delete_outline),\n'
              '            title: const Text(\'Delete for me\'),\n'
              '            onTap: () {\n'
              '              _store.deleteMessage(_chatId, message.id);\n'
              '              Navigator.of(sheetContext).pop();\n'
              '            },\n'
              '          ),\n'
              '        ),\n'
              '      );\n'
              '      return;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    showModalBottomSheet<void>(\n'
              '      context: context,\n'
              '      isScrollControlled: true,\n'
              '      builder: (sheetContext) {\n'
              '        return SafeArea(\n'
              '          child: SingleChildScrollView(\n'
              '            child: Column(\n'
              '              mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,\n'
              '              children: [\n'
              '                _ReactionRow(\n'
              '                  onSelected: (emoji) {\n'
              '                    _react(message.id, emoji);\n'
              '                    Navigator.of(sheetContext).pop();\n'
              '                  },\n'
              '                  onMore: () {\n'
              '                    Navigator.of(sheetContext).pop();\n'
              '                    _pickReactionEmoji(message.id);\n'
              '                  },\n'
              '                ),\n'
              '                const Divider(height: 1),\n'
              '                if (message.text.trim().isNotEmpty &&\n'
              '                    _mayLeaveChat(message) &&\n'
              '                    !QuickReplies.instance.isFull)\n'
              '                  ListTile(\n'
              '                    leading: const Icon(Icons.bolt_outlined),\n'
              '                    title: const Text(\'Save as quick reply\'),\n'
              '                    onTap: () async {\n'
              '                      Navigator.of(sheetContext).pop();\n'
              '                      final saved =\n'
              '                          await QuickReplies.instance.add(message.text);\n'
              '                      if (!mounted) return;\n'
              '                      ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(SnackBar(\n'
              '                          content: Text(saved\n'
              '                              ? \'Saved as a quick reply\'\n'
              '                              : \'That is already saved\')));\n'
              '                    },\n'
              '                  ),\n'
              '                ListTile(\n'
              '                  leading: const Icon(Icons.reply),\n'
              '                  title: const Text(\'Reply\'),\n'
              '                  onTap: () {\n'
              '                    Navigator.of(sheetContext).pop();\n'
              '                    _startReply(message);\n'
              '                  },\n'
              '                ),\n'
              '                // Who has read this — own messages in a group only: in a\n'
              '                // 1:1 the ticks already say everything there is to say,\n'
              '                // and someone else\'s message is not yours to audit.\n'
              '                if (widget.chat.contact.isGroup && message.isMe)\n'
              '                  ListTile(\n'
              '                    leading: const Icon(Icons.done_all),\n'
              '                    title: Text(message.seenBy.isEmpty\n'
              '                        ? \'Seen by nobody yet\'\n'
              '                        : \'Seen by ${message.seenBy.length}\'),\n'
              '                    onTap: () {\n'
              '                      Navigator.of(sheetContext).pop();\n'
              '                      _showSeenBy(message);\n'
              '                    },\n'
              '                  ),\n'
              '                // Groups only, and never from inside a thread: a thread of\n'
              '                // threads is how a group gets a second place to lose a\n'
              '                // conversation. In a 1:1 there is nothing to spare the room\n'
              '                // from — the room is the two of you.\n'
              '                if (widget.chat.contact.isGroup &&\n'
              '                    !_inThread &&\n'
              '                    message.threadRootId == null)\n'
              '                  ListTile(\n'
              '                    leading: const Icon(Icons.forum_outlined),\n'
              '                    title: const Text(\'Reply in thread\'),\n'
              '                    subtitle: const Text(\'Keeps it out of the main group\'),\n'
              '                    onTap: () {\n'
              '                      Navigator.of(sheetContext).pop();\n'
              '                      _openThread(message);\n'
              '                    },\n'
              '                  ),\n'
              '                // A photo forwards as a photo. It used to forward\n'
              '                // `message.text`, which for a picture sent without a caption\n'
              '                // is an empty bubble at the other end.\n'
              '                if ((message.isImage || message.text.trim().isNotEmpty) &&\n'
              '                    _mayLeaveChat(message))\n'
              '                  ListTile(\n'
              '                    leading: const Icon(Icons.shortcut),\n'
              '                    title: const Text(\'Forward\'),\n'
              '                    onTap: () {\n'
              '                      Navigator.of(sheetContext).pop();\n'
              '                      Navigator.of(context).push(\n'
              '                        MaterialPageRoute(\n'
              '                          builder: (_) => ForwardScreen(\n'
              '                              text: message.text,\n'
              '                              imageUrl:\n'
              '                                  message.isImage ? message.imageUrl : null),\n'
              '                        ),\n'
              '                      );\n'
              '                    },\n'
              '                  ),\n'
              '                ListTile(\n'
              '                  leading: const Icon(Icons.check_circle_outline),\n'
              '                  title: const Text(\'Select\'),\n'
              '                  onTap: () {\n'
              '                    Navigator.of(sheetContext).pop();\n'
              '                    _enterSelection(message.id);\n'
              '                  },\n'
              '                ),\n'
              '                Builder(builder: (context) {\n'
              '                  final pinned =\n'
              '                      _store.chatById(_chatId)?.isPinnedMessage(message.id) ??\n'
              '                          false;\n'
              '                  return ListTile(\n'
              '                    leading:\n'
              '                        Icon(pinned ? Icons.push_pin : Icons.push_pin_outlined),\n'
              '                    title: Text(pinned ? \'Unpin\' : \'Pin\'),\n'
              '                    onTap: () {\n'
              '                      Navigator.of(sheetContext).pop();\n'
              '                      if (pinned) {\n'
              '                        _store.unpinMessage(_chatId, message.id);\n'
              '                      } else {\n'
              '                        _tryPin(message);\n'
              '                      }\n'
              '                    },\n'
              '                  );\n'
              '                }),\n'
              '                if (message.isMe && !message.isImage && !message.isVoice)\n'
              '                  ListTile(\n'
              '                    leading: const Icon(Icons.edit_outlined),\n'
              '                    title: const Text(\'Edit\'),\n'
              '                    onTap: () {\n'
              '                      Navigator.of(sheetContext).pop();\n'
              '                      _editMessage(message);\n'
              '                    },\n'
              '                  ),\n'
              '                if (message.text.trim().isNotEmpty && _mayLeaveChat(message))\n'
              '                  ListTile(\n'
              '                    leading: const Icon(Icons.copy),\n'
              '                    title: const Text(\'Copy\'),\n'
              '                    onTap: () {\n'
              '                      Clipboard.setData(ClipboardData(text: message.text));\n'
              '                      Navigator.of(sheetContext).pop();\n'
              '                      ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(\n'
              '                        const SnackBar(content: Text(\'Message copied\')),\n'
              '                      );\n'
              '                    },\n'
              '                  ),\n'
              '                // Translate — on-device only, so it sits on an encrypted chat\n'
              '                // without the text ever leaving the phone.\n'
              '                if (message.text.trim().isNotEmpty && _mayLeaveChat(message))\n'
              '                  ListTile(\n'
              '                    leading: const Icon(Icons.translate),\n'
              '                    title: Text(\n'
              '                        \'Translate to ${TranslateService.instance.targetName()}\'),\n'
              '                    onTap: () {\n'
              '                      Navigator.of(sheetContext).pop();\n'
              '                      _translateMessage(message);\n'
              '                    },\n'
              '                  ),\n'
              '                // Spark: money pinned to something they said, same rails as\n'
              '                // Send money. Their messages only. In a group it pays the\n'
              '                // SENDER — offered only when their digits rode on the\n'
              '                // message, so an old message without them shows no bolt.\n'
              '                if (!message.isMe &&\n'
              '                    !_isNoteToSelf &&\n'
              '                    (!widget.chat.contact.isGroup ||\n'
              '                        message.senderPhone.isNotEmpty) &&\n'
              '                    PaymentService.instance.isConfigured)\n'
              '                  ListTile(\n'
              '                    leading:\n'
              '                        const Icon(Icons.bolt, color: Color(0xFFF7931A)),\n'
              '                    title: const Text(\'Spark\'),\n'
              '                    subtitle: widget.chat.contact.isGroup\n'
              '                        ? Text(\'Send money to \'\n'
              '                            \'${message.senderName.isEmpty ? \'the sender\' : message.senderName}\')\n'
              '                        : null,\n'
              '                    onTap: () {\n'
              '                      Navigator.of(sheetContext).pop();\n'
              '                      _sparkMessage(message);\n'
              '                    },\n'
              '                  ),\n'
              '                ListTile(\n'
              '                  leading: Icon(_store.isStarred(_chatId, message.id)\n'
              '                      ? Icons.star\n'
              '                      : Icons.star_border),\n'
              '                  title: Text(_store.isStarred(_chatId, message.id)\n'
              '                      ? \'Unstar\'\n'
              '                      : \'Star\'),\n'
              '                  onTap: () {\n'
              '                    _store.toggleStar(_chatId, message.id);\n'
              '                    Navigator.of(sheetContext).pop();\n'
              '                  },\n'
              '                ),\n'
              '                if (message.isMe)\n'
              '                  ListTile(\n'
              '                    leading: const Icon(Icons.info_outline),\n'
              '                    title: const Text(\'Message info\'),\n'
              '                    onTap: () {\n'
              '                      Navigator.of(sheetContext).pop();\n'
              '                      _showMessageInfo(message);\n'
              '                    },\n'
              '                  ),\n'
              '                ListTile(\n'
              '                  leading: const Icon(Icons.delete_outline, color: Colors.red),\n'
              '                  title:\n'
              '                      const Text(\'Delete\', style: TextStyle(color: Colors.red)),\n'
              '                  onTap: () {\n'
              '                    Navigator.of(sheetContext).pop();\n'
              '                    _deleteMessage(message);\n'
              '                  },\n'
              '                ),\n'
              '              ],\n'
              '            ),\n'
              '          ),\n'
              '        );\n'
              '      },\n'
              '    );\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  /// Translates a message on-device and shows the result. The text never\n'
              '  /// leaves the phone — see [TranslateService]. Unavailable on the web build\n'
              '  /// and on iPhones below iOS 17.4, which is said plainly rather than failing\n'
              '  /// silently.\n'
              '  Future<void> _translateMessage(Message message) async {\n'
              '    final svc = TranslateService.instance;\n'
              '    final messenger = ScaffoldMessenger.of(context);\n'
              '    if (!await svc.available) {\n'
              '      messenger.showSnackBar(const SnackBar(\n'
              '          content: Text(\n'
              '              \'Translation isn\\\'t available on this device yet.\')));\n'
              '      return;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    final translated = await svc.translate(message.text);\n'
              '    if (!mounted) return;\n'
              '    if (translated == null) {\n'
              '      messenger.showSnackBar(const SnackBar(\n'
              '          content: Text(\'Couldn\\\'t translate that.\')));\n'
              '      return;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    await showModalBottomSheet<void>(\n'
              '      context: context,\n'
              '      showDragHandle: true,\n'
              '      isScrollControlled: true,\n'
              '      builder: (sheetContext) => SafeArea(\n'
              '        child: Padding(\n'
              '          padding: EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(20, 0, 20,\n'
              '              20 + MediaQuery.of(sheetContext).viewInsets.bottom),\n'
              '          child: Column(\n'
              '            mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,\n'
              '            crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,\n'
              '            children: [\n'
              '              Row(\n'
              '                children: [\n'
              '                  const Icon(Icons.translate, size: 18),\n'
              '                  const SizedBox(width: 8),\n'
              '                  Text(\'Translated to ${svc.targetName()}\',\n'
              '                      style: const TextStyle(\n'
              '                          fontWeight: FontWeight.w700, fontSize: 15)),\n'
              '                ],\n'
              '              ),\n'
              '              const SizedBox(height: 12),\n'
              '              SelectableText(translated,\n'
              '                  style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 16, height: 1.35)),\n'
              '              const SizedBox(height: 16),\n'
              '              Text(\'Original\',\n'
              '                  style: TextStyle(\n'
              '                      fontSize: 12,\n'
              '                      fontWeight: FontWeight.w700,\n'
              '                      color: Theme.of(sheetContext)\n'
              '                          .colorScheme\n'
              '                          .onSurfaceVariant)),\n'
              '              const SizedBox(height: 4),\n'
              '              Text(message.text,\n'
              '                  style: TextStyle(\n'
              '                      fontSize: 14,\n'
              '                      color: Theme.of(sheetContext)\n'
              '                          .colorScheme\n'
              '                          .onSurfaceVariant)),\n'
              '              const SizedBox(height: 16),\n'
              '              Align(\n'
              '                alignment: Alignment.centerRight,\n'
              '                child: TextButton.icon(\n'
              '                  onPressed: () {\n'
              '                    Clipboard.setData(ClipboardData(text: translated));\n'
              '                    Navigator.of(sheetContext).pop();\n'
              '                    messenger.showSnackBar(const SnackBar(\n'
              '                        content: Text(\'Translation copied\')));\n'
              '                  },\n'
              '                  icon: const Icon(Icons.copy, size: 16),\n'
              '                  label: const Text(\'Copy\'),\n'
              '                ),\n'
              '              ),\n'
              '              Text(\'Translated on your device — the text never left the phone.\',\n'
              '                  style: TextStyle(\n'
              '                      fontSize: 11.5,\n'
              '                      color: Theme.of(sheetContext)\n'
              '                          .colorScheme\n'
              '                          .onSurfaceVariant)),\n'
              '            ],\n'
              '          ),\n'
              '        ),\n'
              '      ),\n'
              '    );\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  Future<void> _editMessage(Message message) async {\n'
              '    final result = await showAppTextPrompt(\n'
              '      context,\n'
              '      icon: Icons.edit_outlined,\n'
              '      title: \'Edit message\',\n'
              '      initial: message.text,\n'
              '      maxLines: 4,\n'
              '      capitalization: TextCapitalization.sentences,\n'
              '    );\n'
              '    final text = result?.trim();\n'
              '    if (text == null || text.isEmpty || text == message.text) return;\n'
              '    _store.editMessage(_chatId, message.id, text);\n'
              '    for (final phone in _relayPhones()) {\n'
              '      RelayService.instance.sendEdit(phone, message.id, text);\n'
              '    }\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  /// Deletes a message: for your own messages on a real-peer chat, offers to\n'
              '  /// delete it for everyone (removing it on the other device too).\n'
              '  Future<void> _deleteMessage(Message message) async {\n'
              '    // Your own message can be recalled anywhere it was actually delivered —\n'
              '    // a real 1:1 peer or a group (the old real-peer check silently made\n'
              '    // group deletes local-only, so the message lived on for everyone else).\n'
              '    final canDeleteForEveryone =\n'
              '        message.isMe && _relayPhones().isNotEmpty;\n'
              '    if (!canDeleteForEveryone) {\n'
              '      _store.deleteMessage(_chatId, message.id);\n'
              '      return;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    await showModalBottomSheet<void>(\n'
              '      context: context,\n'
              '      builder: (sheetContext) => SafeArea(\n'
              '        child: Column(\n'
              '          mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,\n'
              '          children: [\n'
              '            ListTile(\n'
              '              leading: const Icon(Icons.delete_outline),\n'
              '              title: const Text(\'Delete for me\'),\n'
              '              onTap: () {\n'
              '                _store.deleteMessage(_chatId, message.id);\n'
              '                Navigator.of(sheetContext).pop();\n'
              '              },\n'
              '            ),\n'
              '            ListTile(\n'
              '              leading: const Icon(Icons.delete_forever, color: Colors.red),\n'
              '              title: const Text(\'Delete for everyone\',\n'
              '                  style: TextStyle(color: Colors.red)),\n'
              '              onTap: () {\n'
              '                _store.deleteMessage(_chatId, message.id, forEveryone: true);\n'
              '                for (final phone in _relayPhones()) {\n'
              '                  RelayService.instance.sendDelete(phone, message.id);\n'
              '                }\n'
              '                Navigator.of(sheetContext).pop();\n'
              '              },\n'
              '            ),\n'
              '          ],\n'
              '        ),\n'
              '      ),\n'
              '    );\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  /// Who has read [message], and who hasn\'t yet — the group\'s roster split\n'
              '  /// by the per-reader receipts. Members only ever appear by the names the\n'
              '  /// roster already shows; nobody\'s reading is timestamped.\n'
              '  void _showSeenBy(Message message) {\n'
              '    final me = RelayService.digits(Session.instance.user.value?.phone ?? \'\');\n'
              '    final members = [\n'
              '      for (final m in widget.chat.members)\n'
              '        if (RelayService.digits(m.phone) != me && m.phone.isNotEmpty) m\n'
              '    ];\n'
              '    final seenDigits = message.seenBy.toSet();\n'
              '    final seen = [\n'
              '      for (final m in members)\n'
              '        if (seenDigits.contains(RelayService.digits(m.phone))) m\n'
              '    ];\n'
              '    final notYet = [\n'
              '      for (final m in members)\n'
              '        if (!seenDigits.contains(RelayService.digits(m.phone))) m\n'
              '    ];\n'
              '    showModalBottomSheet<void>(\n'
              '      context: context,\n'
              '      showDragHandle: true,\n'
              '      builder: (sheetContext) => SafeArea(\n'
              '        child: ListView(\n'
              '          shrinkWrap: true,\n'
              '          children: [\n'
              '            if (seen.isNotEmpty) ...[\n'
              '              Padding(\n'
              '                padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(20, 4, 20, 4),\n'
              '                child: Text(\'SEEN BY\',\n'
              '                    style: TextStyle(\n'
              '                        fontSize: 12,\n'
              '                        fontWeight: FontWeight.w700,\n'
              '                        letterSpacing: 0.6,\n'
              '                        color: AppColors.subtle(sheetContext))),\n'
              '              ),\n'
              '              for (final m in seen)\n'
              '                ListTile(\n'
              '                  dense: true,\n'
              '                  leading: UserAvatar(user: m, radius: 17),\n'
              '                  title: Text(m.name,\n'
              '                      maxLines: 1, overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis),\n'
              '                ),\n'
              '            ] else\n'
              '              Padding(\n'
              '                padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(20, 8, 20, 8),\n'
              '                child: Text(\'Nobody has opened this yet.\',\n'
              '                    style: TextStyle(color: AppColors.subtle(sheetContext))),\n'
              '              ),\n'
              '            if (notYet.isNotEmpty) ...[\n'
              '              Padding(\n'
              '                padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(20, 10, 20, 4),\n'
              '                child: Text(\'NOT YET\',\n'
              '                    style: TextStyle(\n'
              '                        fontSize: 12,\n'
              '                        fontWeight: FontWeight.w700,\n'
              '                        letterSpacing: 0.6,\n'
              '                        color: AppColors.subtle(sheetContext))),\n'
              '              ),\n'
              '              for (final m in notYet)\n'
              '                ListTile(\n'
              '                  dense: true,\n'
              '                  leading: UserAvatar(user: m, radius: 17),\n'
              '                  title: Text(m.name,\n'
              '                      maxLines: 1, overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis),\n'
              '                ),\n'
              '            ],\n'
              '            const SizedBox(height: 8),\n'
              '          ],\n'
              '        ),\n'
              '      ),\n'
              '    );\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  /// Toggles a reaction locally and mirrors it to a real peer over the relay.\n'
              '  void _react(String messageId, String emoji) {\n'
              '    Haptics.tap();\n'
              '    _store.toggleReaction(_chatId, messageId, emoji);\n'
              '    ScoreStore.instance.award(ScoreStore.pointsPerReaction);\n'
              '    ScoreStore.instance.recordFlag(\'reacted\');\n'
              '    final phones = _relayPhones();\n'
              '    if (phones.isNotEmpty) {\n'
              '      final present = _store\n'
              '              .chatById(_chatId)\n'
              '              ?.messages\n'
              '              .firstWhere((m) => m.id == messageId)\n'
              '              .reactions\n'
              '              .contains(emoji) ??\n'
              '          false;\n'
              '      for (final phone in phones) {\n'
              '        RelayService.instance\n'
              '            .sendReaction(phone, messageId, emoji, present);\n'
              '      }\n'
              '    }\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  /// Opens the full emoji grid to react to [messageId] with any emoji.\n'
              '  void _pickReactionEmoji(String messageId) {\n'
              '    showModalBottomSheet<void>(\n'
              '      context: context,\n'
              '      builder: (sheetContext) => SafeArea(\n'
              '        child: SizedBox(\n'
              '          height: 320,\n'
              '          child: Column(\n'
              '            children: [\n'
              '              const Padding(\n'
              '                padding: EdgeInsets.all(14),\n'
              '                child: Text(\'React with…\',\n'
              '                    style:\n'
              '                        TextStyle(fontSize: 16, fontWeight: FontWeight.w600)),\n'
              '              ),\n'
              '              Expanded(\n'
              '                child: EmojiPane(\n'
              '                  onPick: (e) {\n'
              '                    Navigator.of(sheetContext).pop();\n'
              '                    _react(messageId, e);\n'
              '                  },\n'
              '                ),\n'
              '              ),\n'
              '            ],\n'
              '          ),\n'
              '        ),\n'
              '      ),\n'
              '    );\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  /// Toggles a ❤️ reaction; when one is added, pops a heart at the tap point.\n'
              '  void _quickReact(Message message) {\n'
              '    _react(message.id, \'❤️\');\n'
              '    final added = _store\n'
              '            .chatById(_chatId)\n'
              '            ?.messages\n'
              '            .firstWhere((m) => m.id == message.id)\n'
              '            .reactions\n'
              '            .contains(\'❤️\') ??\n'
              '        false;\n'
              '    if (added && _lastDoubleTapPos != null) {\n'
              '      _showHeartBurst(_lastDoubleTapPos!);\n'
              '    }\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  void _showHeartBurst(Offset globalPosition) {\n'
              '    final overlay = Overlay.of(context);\n'
              '    final box = overlay.context.findRenderObject() as RenderBox?;\n'
              '    final local = box?.globalToLocal(globalPosition) ?? globalPosition;\n'
              '    late final OverlayEntry entry;\n'
              '    entry = OverlayEntry(\n'
              '      builder: (_) => HeartBurst(\n'
              '        position: local,\n'
              '        onDone: () => entry.remove(),\n'
              '      ),\n'
              '    );\n'
              '    overlay.insert(entry);\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  Future<void> _chooseDisappearing() async {\n'
              '    const options = <String, int>{\n'
              '      \'Off\': 0,\n'
              '      \'1 hour\': 3600,\n'
              '      \'1 day\': 86400,\n'
              '      \'1 week\': 604800,\n'
              '    };\n'
              '    final current = _store.chatById(_chatId)?.disappearingSeconds ?? 0;\n'
              '    final chosen = await showModalBottomSheet<int>(\n'
              '      context: context,\n'
              '      builder: (sheetContext) => SafeArea(\n'
              '        child: Column(\n'
              '          mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,\n'
              '          children: [\n'
              '            const Padding(\n'
              '              padding: EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(20, 18, 20, 6),\n'
              '              child: Row(children: [\n'
              '                Icon(Icons.timer_outlined, size: 20),\n'
              '                SizedBox(width: 10),\n'
              '                Text(\'Disappearing messages\',\n'
              '                    style:\n'
              '                        TextStyle(fontSize: 16, fontWeight: FontWeight.w600)),\n'
              '              ]),\n'
              '            ),\n'
              '            const Padding(\n'
              '              padding: EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(20, 0, 20, 10),\n'
              '              child: Text(\n'
              '                \'New messages in this chat will be deleted from this device \'\n'
              '                \'after the selected time.\',\n'
              '                style: TextStyle(color: Colors.grey, fontSize: 13),\n'
              '              ),\n'
              '            ),\n'
              '            for (final entry in options.entries)\n'
              '              ListTile(\n'
              '                title: Text(entry.key),\n'
              '                trailing: entry.value == current\n'
              '                    ? Icon(Icons.check,\n'
              '                        color: Theme.of(sheetContext).colorScheme.primary)\n'
              '                    : null,\n'
              '                onTap: () => Navigator.of(sheetContext).pop(entry.value),\n'
              '              ),\n'
              '          ],\n'
              '        ),\n'
              '      ),\n'
              '    );\n'
              '    if (chosen == null || !mounted) return;\n'
              '    _store.setDisappearing(_chatId, chosen);\n'
              '    setState(() {});\n'
              '    final label =\n'
              '        options.entries.firstWhere((e) => e.value == chosen).key;\n'
              '    ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(SnackBar(\n'
              '      content: Text(chosen == 0\n'
              '          ? \'Disappearing messages off\'\n'
              '          : \'Disappearing messages: $label\'),\n'
              '    ));\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  /// Pull-to-refresh: rebuild the live subscription and drain the offline\n'
              '  /// mailbox, so "is something stuck?" has an answer that is always safe to\n'
              '  /// reach for. A no-op (with a beat of spinner) when the relay is off.\n'
              '  Future<void> _refreshChat() async {\n'
              '    if (!RelayConfig.isEnabled) {\n'
              '      await Future<void>.delayed(const Duration(milliseconds: 400));\n'
              '      return;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    await RelayService.instance.wake();\n'
              '    await RelayService.instance.fetchMailbox();\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  void _startCall({required bool video}) {\n'
              '    if (_isNoteToSelf) return; // nobody on the other end to ring\n'
              '    if (widget.chat.contact.isGroup) {\n'
              '      final chat = _store.chatById(_chatId) ?? widget.chat;\n'
              '      CallService.instance.startGroupCall(chat, video: video);\n'
              '      return;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    CallService.instance.startOutgoing(widget.chat.contact, video: video);\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  /// A WhatsApp-style "Message info" dialog: when it was sent and how far it\n'
              '  /// got (sent → delivered → read), plus edit details when applicable.\n'
              '  void _showMessageInfo(Message message) {\n'
              '    final statusLabel = switch (message.status) {\n'
              '      MessageStatus.read => \'Read\',\n'
              '      MessageStatus.delivered => \'Delivered\',\n'
              '      MessageStatus.sent => \'Sent\',\n'
              '      _ => \'Pending\',\n'
              '    };\n'
              '    showDialog<void>(\n'
              '      context: context,\n'
              '      builder: (dialogContext) => AlertDialog(\n'
              '        title: const Text(\'Message info\'),\n'
              '        content: Column(\n'
              '          mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,\n'
              '          crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,\n'
              '          children: [\n'
              '            _infoRow(Icons.schedule,\n'
              '                \'Sent ${DateFormatter.messageTime(message.time)}\'),\n'
              '            const SizedBox(height: 10),\n'
              '            _infoRow(\n'
              '              message.status == MessageStatus.read\n'
              '                  ? Icons.done_all\n'
              '                  : message.status == MessageStatus.delivered\n'
              '                      ? Icons.done_all\n'
              '                      : Icons.done,\n'
              '              \'Status: $statusLabel\',\n'
              '            ),\n'
              '            if (message.edited) ...[\n'
              '              const SizedBox(height: 10),\n'
              '              _infoRow(Icons.edit_outlined, \'Edited\'),\n'
              '            ],\n'
              '            if (message.viewOnce) ...[\n'
              '              const SizedBox(height: 10),\n'
              '              _infoRow(\n'
              '                  Icons.timer_outlined,\n'
              '                  message.viewOnceOpened\n'
              '                      ? \'View once · opened\'\n'
              '                      : \'View once · not opened yet\'),\n'
              '            ],\n'
              '          ],\n'
              '        ),\n'
              '        actions: [\n'
              '          TextButton(\n'
              '            onPressed: () => Navigator.of(dialogContext).pop(),\n'
              '            child: const Text(\'Close\'),\n'
              '          ),\n'
              '        ],\n'
              '      ),\n'
              '    );\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  Widget _infoRow(IconData icon, String text) => Row(\n'
              '        children: [\n'
              '          Icon(icon, size: 18, color: AppColors.subtle(context)),\n'
              '          const SizedBox(width: 10),\n'
              '          Expanded(child: Text(text)),\n'
              '        ],\n'
              '      );\n'
              '\n'
              '  /// Whether tapping an image should open the viewer. A spent view-once photo\n'
              '  /// is no longer openable by the recipient.\n'
              '  bool _canOpenImage(Message m) {\n'
              '    if (!m.isImage) return false;\n'
              '    if (!m.viewOnce) return true;\n'
              '    if (m.isMe) return true; // the sender can still review what they sent\n'
              '    return !m.viewOnceOpened;\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  /// A ghost text opens like a view-once photo: the sender can re-read what\n'
              '  /// they wrote; the recipient gets exactly one viewing.\n'
              '  bool _canOpenGhost(Message m) {\n'
              '    if (!m.viewOnce || m.isImage) return false;\n'
              '    if (m.isMe) return true;\n'
              '    return !m.viewOnceOpened;\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  Future<void> _openGhost(Message message) async {\n'
              '    // Refused, not blanked-after: opening it during a recording would read\n'
              '    // the message into the capture before the viewer could hide anything.\n'
              '    if (!message.isMe && ScreenshotWatch.instance.capturing.value) {\n'
              '      ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(const SnackBar(\n'
              '          content: Text(\'Ghost messages stay hidden while the screen is \'\n'
              '              \'being recorded or mirrored.\')));\n'
              '      return;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    final consume =\n'
              '        message.viewOnce && !message.isMe && !message.viewOnceOpened;\n'
              '    await Navigator.of(context).push(\n'
              '      MaterialPageRoute(\n'
              '        builder: (_) => GhostViewScreen(\n'
              '          text: message.text,\n'
              '          senderName: message.isMe ? \'You\' : widget.chat.contact.name,\n'
              '          onScreenshot: () {\n'
              '            _store.noteScreenshot(_chatId, byMe: true, ghost: true);\n'
              '            final myName = AppState.profile.value.name;\n'
              '            for (final phone in _relayPhones()) {\n'
              '              RelayService.instance.sendGhostShotNotice(phone);\n'
              '              // A kept ghost message is exactly what its sender most wants\n'
              '              // to hear about, whether or not their app is open.\n'
              '              PushService.instance.notify(phone,\n'
              '                  title: myName.isEmpty ? \'Screenshot\' : myName,\n'
              '                  body: \'Screenshotted your ghost message!\');\n'
              '            }\n'
              '          },\n'
              '        ),\n'
              '      ),\n'
              '    );\n'
              '    if (consume) {\n'
              '      _store.markViewOnceOpened(_chatId, message.id);\n'
              '      for (final phone in _relayPhones()) {\n'
              '        RelayService.instance.sendViewOnceOpened(phone, message.id);\n'
              '      }\n'
              '    }\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  /// Composes a ghost message: typed in its own sheet rather than the\n'
              '  /// composer, so the words never sit in a field that autocorrect, drafts or\n'
              '  /// the send button treat as an ordinary message.\n'
              '  Future<void> _composeGhost() async {\n'
              '    final controller = TextEditingController();\n'
              '    final text = await showModalBottomSheet<String>(\n'
              '      context: context,\n'
              '      isScrollControlled: true,\n'
              '      showDragHandle: true,\n'
              '      builder: (sheetContext) => Padding(\n'
              '        padding: EdgeInsets.only(\n'
              '            left: 20,\n'
              '            right: 20,\n'
              '            bottom: MediaQuery.of(sheetContext).viewInsets.bottom + 20),\n'
              '        child: Column(\n'
              '          mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,\n'
              '          crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,\n'
              '          children: [\n'
              '            const Text(\'Ghost message\',\n'
              '                style: TextStyle(fontSize: 17, fontWeight: FontWeight.w700)),\n'
              '            const SizedBox(height: 6),\n'
              '            Text(\n'
              '              // The promise this feature can actually keep, said up front:\n'
              '              // one view, hidden from recordings, screenshots announced —\n'
              '              // never "cannot be screenshotted", which iOS does not offer.\n'
              '              \'Can be read once, then it\\\'s gone. It stays hidden while a \'\n'
              '              \'screen recording is running, and screenshots can\\\'t be \'\n'
              '              \'blocked — both of you see a note if one is taken.\',\n'
              '              style: TextStyle(\n'
              '                  fontSize: 12.5, color: AppColors.subtle(sheetContext)),\n'
              '            ),\n'
              '            const SizedBox(height: 14),\n'
              '            TextField(\n'
              '              controller: controller,\n'
              '              autofocus: true,\n'
              '              minLines: 1,\n'
              '              maxLines: 5,\n'
              '              textCapitalization: TextCapitalization.sentences,\n'
              '              decoration: const InputDecoration(\n'
              '                hintText: \'Say it once…\',\n'
              '                border: OutlineInputBorder(),\n'
              '              ),\n'
              '              onSubmitted: (v) => Navigator.of(sheetContext).pop(v),\n'
              '            ),\n'
              '            const SizedBox(height: 12),\n'
              '            FilledButton(\n'
              '              onPressed: () =>\n'
              '                  Navigator.of(sheetContext).pop(controller.text),\n'
              '              child: const Text(\'Send ghost message\'),\n'
              '            ),\n'
              '          ],\n'
              '        ),\n'
              '      ),\n'
              '    );\n'
              '    controller.dispose();\n'
              '    if (text == null || text.trim().isEmpty) return;\n'
              '    if (!mounted || !await _confirmRecipient()) return;\n'
              '    final now = DateTime.now();\n'
              '    _deliver(Message(\n'
              '      id: \'ghost_${now.microsecondsSinceEpoch}\',\n'
              '      text: text.trim(),\n'
              '      time: now,\n'
              '      isMe: true,\n'
              '      status: MessageStatus.sent,\n'
              '      viewOnce: true,\n'
              '    ));\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  Future<void> _openImage(Message message) async {\n'
              '    // The single viewing must not be spent into a recording — same rule as a\n'
              '    // ghost text.\n'
              '    if (message.viewOnce &&\n'
              '        !message.isMe &&\n'
              '        ScreenshotWatch.instance.capturing.value) {\n'
              '      ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(const SnackBar(\n'
              '          content: Text(\'View-once photos stay hidden while the screen is \'\n'
              '              \'being recorded or mirrored.\')));\n'
              '      return;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    // A received "view once" photo is consumed after this single viewing.\n'
              '    final consume =\n'
              '        message.viewOnce && !message.isMe && !message.viewOnceOpened;\n'
              '    await Navigator.of(context).push(\n'
              '      MaterialPageRoute(\n'
              '        builder: (_) => ImageViewScreen(\n'
              '          message: message,\n'
              '          senderName: widget.chat.contact.name,\n'
              '        ),\n'
              '      ),\n'
              '    );\n'
              '    if (consume) {\n'
              '      _store.markViewOnceOpened(_chatId, message.id);\n'
              '      for (final phone in _relayPhones()) {\n'
              '        RelayService.instance.sendViewOnceOpened(phone, message.id);\n'
              '      }\n'
              '    }\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  /// Whether this conversation is the private notes chat. Nothing leaves it,\n'
              '  /// so there is nobody to pay.\n'
              '  bool get _isNoteToSelf => widget.chat.contact.id == \'self\';\n'
              '\n'
              '  /// The members of a group who could actually be paid: real, number-identified\n'
              '  /// people, never yourself and never the group itself.\n'
              '  List<AppUser> get _payableGroupMembers {\n'
              '    final me = AppState.profile.value;\n'
              '    return [\n'
              '      for (final u in widget.chat.members)\n'
              '        if (u.id != me.id && u.id != \'me\' && u.id != \'self\' && _isRealPeer(u))\n'
              '          u\n'
              '    ];\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  /// Asks which member of a group to pay, then makes them confirm it.\n'
              '  ///\n'
              '  /// A group has no single "the other person", so sending to the chat would\n'
              '  /// have to guess — and guessing wrong here moves real money to the wrong\n'
              '  /// person. Two deliberate steps: pick a name, then confirm that name.\n'
              '  Future<AppUser?> _pickGroupRecipient() async {\n'
              '    final members = _payableGroupMembers;\n'
              '    if (members.isEmpty) {\n'
              '      ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(const SnackBar(\n'
              '          content: Text(\'No one in this group has set up payments yet\')));\n'
              '      return null;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    final picked = await showModalBottomSheet<AppUser>(\n'
              '      context: context,\n'
              '      showDragHandle: true,\n'
              '      builder: (sheetContext) => SafeArea(\n'
              '        child: Column(\n'
              '          mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,\n'
              '          children: [\n'
              '            const Padding(\n'
              '              padding: EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(20, 4, 20, 12),\n'
              '              child: Text(\'Who are you paying?\',\n'
              '                  style:\n'
              '                      TextStyle(fontSize: 18, fontWeight: FontWeight.w700)),\n'
              '            ),\n'
              '            for (final m in members)\n'
              '              ListTile(\n'
              '                leading: UserAvatar(user: m, radius: 20),\n'
              '                title: Text(m.name),\n'
              '                subtitle: Text(m.phone),\n'
              '                onTap: () => Navigator.pop(sheetContext, m),\n'
              '              ),\n'
              '            const SizedBox(height: 8),\n'
              '          ],\n'
              '        ),\n'
              '      ),\n'
              '    );\n'
              '    if (picked == null || !mounted) return null;\n'
              '\n'
              '    // Naming them again, on their own, is the whole point — a mis-tap in a\n'
              '    // list of names shouldn\'t be enough to move money.\n'
              '    final ok = await showAppConfirmDialog(\n'
              '      context,\n'
              '      icon: Icons.person_outline,\n'
              '      title: \'Pay ${picked.name}?\',\n'
              '      message: \'Money will go to ${picked.name} (${picked.phone}). \'\n'
              '          \'Check this is the right person — a sent payment cannot be \'\n'
              '          \'reversed from here.\',\n'
              '      confirmLabel: \'Yes, ${picked.name}\',\n'
              '      cancelLabel: \'Back\',\n'
              '    );\n'
              '    if (!ok || !mounted) return null;\n'
              '    return picked;\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  /// Sends money to a person via Stripe Connect. Opens the amount sheet,\n'
              '  /// creates a direct PaymentIntent on their connected account, presents the\n'
              '  /// native payment sheet, and on success drops a receipt into the\n'
              '  /// conversation.\n'
              '  Future<void> _handleSendMoney() async {\n'
              '    final svc = PaymentService.instance;\n'
              '    if (!svc.isConfigured) {\n'
              '      _showComingSoon(context, \'Payments (add your Stripe key to enable)\');\n'
              '      return;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    if (!svc.canSendOnThisDevice) {\n'
              '      _showComingSoon(context, \'Sending money (use the mobile app)\');\n'
              '      return;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    // The same gate the Wallet screen is behind. Sending money from a chat is\n'
              '    // the wallet\'s own capability reached another way, so guarding one screen\n'
              '    // and not this would be guarding the door and leaving the window.\n'
              '    if (!IdentityVerification.instance.allowsTrusted) {\n'
              '      ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(SnackBar(\n'
              '        content: const Text(\'Verify your ID to send money.\'),\n'
              '        action: SnackBarAction(\n'
              '          label: \'Verify\',\n'
              '          onPressed: () => Navigator.of(context)\n'
              '              .push(MaterialPageRoute(builder: (_) => const ScoreScreen())),\n'
              '        ),\n'
              '      ));\n'
              '      return;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    // Not even in test mode: there is no second party in your own notes, so\n'
              '    // offering to pay them is nonsense however the payment is simulated.\n'
              '    if (_isNoteToSelf) {\n'
              '      ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(const SnackBar(\n'
              '          content: Text(\'You can\\\'t send money to yourself\')));\n'
              '      return;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '\n'
              '    // A group is not a recipient. Resolve one person, deliberately.\n'
              '    var recipient = widget.chat.contact;\n'
              '    if (recipient.isGroup) {\n'
              '      final picked = await _pickGroupRecipient();\n'
              '      if (picked == null || !mounted) return;\n'
              '      recipient = picked;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '\n'
              '    // Test mode simulates locally, so it works with any contact.\n'
              '    if (!svc.testMode.value && !_isRealPeer(recipient)) {\n'
              '      _showComingSoon(context, \'Payments (needs a real contact)\');\n'
              '      return;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    // Said BEFORE the amount sheet, not after: a person who typed a number\n'
              '    // and confirmed it has already committed, and "they haven\'t set up\n'
              '    // payments" is a fact that was knowable up front. The server re-checks\n'
              '    // authoritatively either way.\n'
              '    if (!svc.testMode.value && !await svc.canReceive(recipient.phone)) {\n'
              '      if (mounted) {\n'
              '        ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(SnackBar(\n'
              '            content: Text(\'${recipient.name} hasn\\\'t set up payments, so \'\n'
              '                \'money can\\\'t reach them yet.\')));\n'
              '      }\n'
              '      return;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    if (!mounted) return;\n'
              '    if (!widget.chat.contact.isGroup && !await _confirmRecipient()) return;\n'
              '    if (!mounted) return;\n'
              '    final result = await showModalBottomSheet<({int cents, String note, bool acknowledged})>(\n'
              '      context: context,\n'
              '      isScrollControlled: true,\n'
              '      builder: (_) => PaymentAmountSheet(peerName: recipient.name),\n'
              '    );\n'
              '    if (result == null || result.cents <= 0 || !mounted) return;\n'
              '\n'
              '    // A large amount gets one more look, naming both the person and the\n'
              '    // number. The sheet\'s checkbox covers "I know this is final"; this covers\n'
              '    // "I meant this many zeros", which is the other way a send goes wrong.\n'
              '    if (result.cents >= PaymentEconomics.confirmTwiceAboveCents) {\n'
              '      final sure = await showAppConfirmDialog(\n'
              '        context,\n'
              '        icon: Icons.warning_amber_rounded,\n'
              '        title: \'Send \\$${(result.cents / 100).toStringAsFixed(2)}?\',\n'
              '        message: \'${recipient.name} will receive \'\n'
              '            \'\\$${(result.cents / 100).toStringAsFixed(2)}. \'\n'
              '            \'This cannot be undone from here.\',\n'
              '        confirmLabel: \'Yes, send it\',\n'
              '        cancelLabel: \'Go back\',\n'
              '        destructive: true,\n'
              '      );\n'
              '      if (!sure || !mounted) return;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '\n'
              '    await _payRecipient(recipient,\n'
              '        cents: result.cents,\n'
              '        note: result.note,\n'
              '        acknowledged: result.acknowledged);\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  /// The payment itself, shared by Send money, Spark and paying a request:\n'
              '  /// the optimistic pending receipt in the chat, the real Stripe transfer,\n'
              '  /// and the settle loop that keeps the receipt honest about what actually\n'
              '  /// happened. [onSettled] hears every status the receipt takes, so a\n'
              '  /// request bubble can flip to paid only once the money really moved.\n'
              '  Future<void> _payRecipient(AppUser recipient,\n'
              '      {required int cents,\n'
              '      required String note,\n'
              '      required bool acknowledged,\n'
              '      void Function(String status)? onSettled}) async {\n'
              '    final svc = PaymentService.instance;\n'
              '    final messenger = ScaffoldMessenger.of(context);\n'
              '    final phone = recipient.phone;\n'
              '\n'
              '    // Trusted-location protection: a send from outside a trusted place (or\n'
              '    // with the location unreadable) needs the second-factor PIN first, unless\n'
              '    // the recipient is a trusted contact. Runs BEFORE the optimistic receipt\n'
              '    // so a cancel leaves no phantom pending payment in the chat.\n'
              '    final decision = await PaymentSecurityStore.instance.assess(phone);\n'
              '    if (decision.needsStepUp) {\n'
              '      if (!mounted) return;\n'
              '      final passed = await requestPaymentStepUp(context,\n'
              '          reason: decision, recipientName: recipient.name);\n'
              '      if (!passed) {\n'
              '        if (mounted) {\n'
              '          messenger.showSnackBar(const SnackBar(\n'
              '              content: Text(\'Send cancelled — it needs verification.\')));\n'
              '        }\n'
              '        return;\n'
              '      }\n'
              '    }\n'
              '\n'
              '    final now = DateTime.now();\n'
              '    final payId = \'pay_${now.microsecondsSinceEpoch}\';\n'
              '\n'
              '    // Drop an optimistic "pending" receipt right away and relay it, so the\n'
              '    // recipient sees the payment as pending while it\'s confirmed.\n'
              '    _deliver(Message(\n'
              '      id: payId,\n'
              '      // In a group the receipt is visible to everyone, so it has to say who\n'
              '      // was paid — otherwise it reads as a payment to the room.\n'
              '      text: widget.chat.contact.isGroup\n'
              '          ? (note.isEmpty\n'
              '              ? \'To ${recipient.name}\'\n'
              '              : \'To ${recipient.name} — $note\')\n'
              '          : note,\n'
              '      time: now,\n'
              '      isMe: true,\n'
              '      status: MessageStatus.sent,\n'
              '      isPayment: true,\n'
              '      paymentAmountCents: cents,\n'
              '      paymentCurrency: svc.sendCurrency.value,\n'
              '      paymentStatus: \'pending\',\n'
              '    ));\n'
              '\n'
              '    void settle(String status) {\n'
              '      _store.setPaymentStatus(_chatId, payId, status);\n'
              '      RelayService.instance.sendPaymentStatus(phone, payId, status);\n'
              '      onSettled?.call(status);\n'
              '    }\n'
              '\n'
              '    try {\n'
              '      final ok = await svc.sendMoney(\n'
              '        toPhone: phone,\n'
              '        amountCents: cents,\n'
              '        note: note,\n'
              '        acknowledged: acknowledged,\n'
              '        // The trusted-place check + PIN ran above; tell the funnel so its\n'
              '        // backstop doesn\'t ask the location a second time.\n'
              '        stepUpVerified: true,\n'
              '      );\n'
              '      // The sheet returning true means the card authorised, not that it was\n'
              '      // charged: capture waits on the cardholder check. Saying "paid" here\n'
              '      // would be a lie for a card that is about to be refused.\n'
              '      if (!ok) {\n'
              '        settle(\'failed\');\n'
              '        return;\n'
              '      }\n'
              '      settle(\'pending\');\n'
              '      final intentId = svc.lastPaymentIntentId;\n'
              '      final outcome = intentId.isEmpty\n'
              '          ? \'pending\'\n'
              '          : await svc.awaitSettlement(intentId);\n'
              '      settle(switch (outcome) {\n'
              '        \'succeeded\' || \'paid\' => \'paid\',\n'
              '        \'pending\' => \'pending\',\n'
              '        _ => \'failed\',\n'
              '      });\n'
              '      if (outcome.startsWith(\'blocked_\') && mounted) {\n'
              '        messenger.showSnackBar(SnackBar(\n'
              '          content: Text(outcome == \'blocked_prepaid\'\n'
              '              ? \'Prepaid cards can\\\'t be used to send money.\'\n'
              '              : \'That card has to be in your own name.\'),\n'
              '        ));\n'
              '      }\n'
              '    } on PaymentException catch (e) {\n'
              '      settle(\'failed\');\n'
              '      messenger.showSnackBar(SnackBar(\n'
              '        content: Text(switch (e.code) {\n'
              '          \'receiver_not_onboarded\' =>\n'
              '            \'${widget.chat.contact.name} hasn\\\'t set up payments yet\',\n'
              '          // Say why, plainly. A silent failure here reads as a bug and\n'
              '          // invites another attempt.\n'
              '          \'sender_banned\' =>\n'
              '            \'Sending money is blocked on this account after a chargeback.\',\n'
              '          \'identity_required\' =>\n'
              '            \'Verify your identity before sending money — Settings → Get \'\n'
              '                \'verified.\',\n'
              '          \'recipient_not_accepting\' =>\n'
              '            \'${recipient.name} isn\\\'t accepting payments.\',\n'
              '          \'daily_limit_reached\' =>\n'
              '            \'You\\\'ve reached your daily sending limit. Raise it in \'\n'
              '                \'Wallet → Payment controls (a higher limit starts tomorrow).\',\n'
              '          \'over_send_limit\' =>\n'
              '            \'That\\\'s more than your limit for a single transfer. Change it \'\n'
              '                \'in Wallet → Payment controls.\',\n'
              '          \'payments_paused\' =>\n'
              '            \'Your payments are paused. Turn that off in Wallet → Payment \'\n'
              '                \'controls.\',\n'
              '          // A spark whose post was deleted between the tap and the charge.\n'
              '          \'post_not_found\' =>\n'
              '            \'That post is gone, so its spark has nowhere to go.\',\n'
              '          \'bad_currency\' =>\n'
              '            \'That currency isn\\\'t supported. Pick another in Wallet → \'\n'
              '                \'Payment controls.\',\n'
              '          \'parental_locked\' =>\n'
              '            \'Payments are turned off by parental controls on this phone.\',\n'
              '          _ => \'Payment failed: ${e.code}\',\n'
              '        }),\n'
              '      ));\n'
              '    } catch (_) {\n'
              '      settle(\'failed\');\n'
              '      messenger.showSnackBar(\n'
              '          const SnackBar(content: Text(\'Payment could not be completed\')));\n'
              '    }\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  /// Sparks whoever sent [message] — the same real transfer as Send money,\n'
              '  /// at one-tap amounts, pinned to something they said. Offered on THEIR\n'
              '  /// messages only (sparking yourself is nonsense). In a group the money\n'
              '  /// goes to the message\'s SENDER, not the room: their digits ride on the\n'
              '  /// message (senderPhone), so a group message from a build that carried\n'
              '  /// none simply doesn\'t offer the bolt.\n'
              '  Future<void> _sparkMessage(Message message) async {\n'
              '    final svc = PaymentService.instance;\n'
              '    if (!svc.isConfigured ||\n'
              '        (!svc.canSendOnThisDevice && !svc.testMode.value)) {\n'
              '      _showComingSoon(context, \'Payments\');\n'
              '      return;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    if (!IdentityVerification.instance.allowsTrusted) {\n'
              '      ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(SnackBar(\n'
              '        content: const Text(\'Verify your ID to send money.\'),\n'
              '        action: SnackBarAction(\n'
              '          label: \'Verify\',\n'
              '          onPressed: () => Navigator.of(context)\n'
              '              .push(MaterialPageRoute(builder: (_) => const ScoreScreen())),\n'
              '        ),\n'
              '      ));\n'
              '      return;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    final recipient = _sparkRecipientFor(message);\n'
              '    if (recipient == null) return;\n'
              '    if (!svc.testMode.value && !await svc.canReceive(recipient.phone)) {\n'
              '      if (mounted) {\n'
              '        ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(SnackBar(\n'
              '            content: Text(\'${recipient.name} hasn\\\'t set up payments, so \'\n'
              '                \'sparks can\\\'t reach them yet.\')));\n'
              '      }\n'
              '      return;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    if (!mounted) return;\n'
              '    final cents = await showSparkSheet(context, toLabel: recipient.name);\n'
              '    if (cents == null || cents <= 0 || !mounted) return;\n'
              '    await _payRecipient(recipient,\n'
              '        cents: cents, note: \'Spark ⚡\', acknowledged: true);\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  /// Who a spark on [message] pays: the conversation\'s contact in a 1:1, the\n'
              '  /// message\'s sender in a group — resolved from the roster when the digits\n'
              '  /// match a member (their real card), built from the message when not.\n'
              '  AppUser? _sparkRecipientFor(Message message) {\n'
              '    if (!widget.chat.contact.isGroup) return widget.chat.contact;\n'
              '    final digits = message.senderPhone.replaceAll(RegExp(r\'\\D\'), \'\');\n'
              '    if (digits.isEmpty) return null;\n'
              '    for (final m in widget.chat.members) {\n'
              '      if (m.phone.replaceAll(RegExp(r\'\\D\'), \'\') == digits) return m;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    return AppUser(\n'
              '      id: \'u_$digits\',\n'
              '      name: message.senderName.isEmpty ? \'Member\' : message.senderName,\n'
              '      avatarColor: \'#7A5CFF\',\n'
              '      phone: digits,\n'
              '    );\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  /// A payment request from the other side that is still waiting for an\n'
              '  /// answer — the one payment bubble a tap acts on. 1:1 only: a group\n'
              '  /// request has no single payer, and the contact is not a person to pay.\n'
              '  bool _canAnswerRequest(Message m) =>\n'
              '      m.isPayment &&\n'
              '      m.isPaymentRequest &&\n'
              '      !m.isMe &&\n'
              '      !widget.chat.contact.isGroup &&\n'
              '      (m.paymentStatus == \'requested\' || m.paymentStatus.isEmpty);\n'
              '\n'
              '  /// Asks the other person for money. Nothing is charged here — the request\n'
              '  /// is only a message, and a charge exists only if they tap Pay on it.\n'
              '  /// That is also why it needs none of Send money\'s gates: asking moves\n'
              '  /// nothing, and every check runs on the payer\'s side when they answer.\n'
              '  Future<void> _requestMoney() async {\n'
              '    final svc = PaymentService.instance;\n'
              '    if (!svc.isConfigured ||\n'
              '        (!svc.canSendOnThisDevice && !svc.testMode.value)) {\n'
              '      _showComingSoon(context, \'Payments\');\n'
              '      return;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    final result = await showModalBottomSheet<\n'
              '        ({int cents, String note, bool acknowledged})>(\n'
              '      context: context,\n'
              '      isScrollControlled: true,\n'
              '      builder: (_) => PaymentAmountSheet(\n'
              '          peerName: widget.chat.contact.name, requestMode: true),\n'
              '    );\n'
              '    if (result == null || result.cents <= 0 || !mounted) return;\n'
              '    final now = DateTime.now();\n'
              '    _deliver(Message(\n'
              '      id: \'req_${now.microsecondsSinceEpoch}\',\n'
              '      text: result.note,\n'
              '      time: now,\n'
              '      isMe: true,\n'
              '      status: MessageStatus.sent,\n'
              '      isPayment: true,\n'
              '      isPaymentRequest: true,\n'
              '      paymentAmountCents: result.cents,\n'
              '      paymentCurrency: svc.sendCurrency.value,\n'
              '      paymentStatus: \'requested\',\n'
              '    ));\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  /// The receiving end of a payment request. Pay runs the same rails as Send\n'
              '  /// money — verification, fees grossed up, the real Stripe charge — and\n'
              '  /// Decline answers without moving anything. Either answer flips the\n'
              '  /// request bubble on both phones over the same \'payst\' event a receipt\n'
              '  /// already uses, keyed by the request message\'s id.\n'
              '  Future<void> _answerPaymentRequest(Message m) async {\n'
              '    final svc = PaymentService.instance;\n'
              '    final recipient = widget.chat.contact;\n'
              '    final amount = \'\\$${(m.paymentAmountCents / 100).toStringAsFixed(2)}\';\n'
              '    final answer = await showModalBottomSheet<String>(\n'
              '      context: context,\n'
              '      showDragHandle: true,\n'
              '      builder: (sheetContext) => SafeArea(\n'
              '        child: Column(\n'
              '          mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,\n'
              '          children: [\n'
              '            ListTile(\n'
              '              leading:\n'
              '                  const Icon(Icons.payments_rounded, color: Color(0xFF12B76A)),\n'
              '              title: Text(\'Pay $amount\'),\n'
              '              subtitle: Text(m.text.trim().isEmpty\n'
              '                  ? \'To ${recipient.name}\'\n'
              '                  : \'To ${recipient.name} — ${m.text.trim()}\'),\n'
              '              onTap: () => Navigator.pop(sheetContext, \'pay\'),\n'
              '            ),\n'
              '            ListTile(\n'
              '              leading: const Icon(Icons.close, color: Colors.red),\n'
              '              title: const Text(\'Decline\'),\n'
              '              subtitle: const Text(\'They\\\'ll see the request was declined\'),\n'
              '              onTap: () => Navigator.pop(sheetContext, \'decline\'),\n'
              '            ),\n'
              '          ],\n'
              '        ),\n'
              '      ),\n'
              '    );\n'
              '    if (answer == null || !mounted) return;\n'
              '    if (answer == \'decline\') {\n'
              '      _store.setPaymentStatus(_chatId, m.id, \'declined\');\n'
              '      RelayService.instance\n'
              '          .sendPaymentStatus(recipient.phone, m.id, \'declined\');\n'
              '      return;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    // Paying is sending money, so it carries every gate sending carries.\n'
              '    if (!svc.isConfigured ||\n'
              '        (!svc.canSendOnThisDevice && !svc.testMode.value)) {\n'
              '      _showComingSoon(context, \'Payments\');\n'
              '      return;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    if (!IdentityVerification.instance.allowsTrusted) {\n'
              '      ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(SnackBar(\n'
              '        content: const Text(\'Verify your ID to send money.\'),\n'
              '        action: SnackBarAction(\n'
              '          label: \'Verify\',\n'
              '          onPressed: () => Navigator.of(context)\n'
              '              .push(MaterialPageRoute(builder: (_) => const ScoreScreen())),\n'
              '        ),\n'
              '      ));\n'
              '      return;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    if (!svc.testMode.value && !await svc.canReceive(recipient.phone)) {\n'
              '      if (mounted) {\n'
              '        ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(SnackBar(\n'
              '            content: Text(\'${recipient.name} hasn\\\'t set up payments, so \'\n'
              '                \'money can\\\'t reach them yet.\')));\n'
              '      }\n'
              '      return;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    if (!mounted) return;\n'
              '    // The confirm carries what the acknowledgement checkbox carries on the\n'
              '    // amount sheet: the total with fees, and that it is final.\n'
              '    final total = PaymentEconomics.grossUpCents(m.paymentAmountCents);\n'
              '    final sure = await showAppConfirmDialog(\n'
              '      context,\n'
              '      icon: Icons.payments_rounded,\n'
              '      title: \'Pay $amount?\',\n'
              '      message: \'${recipient.name} receives the full $amount; with fees you \'\n'
              '          \'pay \\$${(total / 100).toStringAsFixed(2)}. This goes straight to \'\n'
              '          \'them and cannot be reversed from here.\',\n'
              '      confirmLabel: \'Pay\',\n'
              '      cancelLabel: \'Go back\',\n'
              '    );\n'
              '    if (!sure || !mounted) return;\n'
              '    await _payRecipient(recipient,\n'
              '        cents: m.paymentAmountCents,\n'
              '        note: m.text.trim(),\n'
              '        acknowledged: true,\n'
              '        onSettled: (status) {\n'
              '          if (status != \'paid\') return;\n'
              '          _store.setPaymentStatus(_chatId, m.id, \'paid\');\n'
              '          RelayService.instance.sendPaymentStatus(recipient.phone, m.id, \'paid\');\n'
              '        });\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  /// Composes and sends a poll into the conversation.\n'
              '  Future<void> _handleCreatePoll() async {\n'
              '    final result =\n'
              '        await showModalBottomSheet<({String question, List<String> options})>(\n'
              '      context: context,\n'
              '      isScrollControlled: true,\n'
              '      builder: (_) => const PollComposerSheet(),\n'
              '    );\n'
              '    if (result == null || !mounted) return;\n'
              '    final now = DateTime.now();\n'
              '    _deliver(Message(\n'
              '      id: \'poll_${now.microsecondsSinceEpoch}\',\n'
              '      text: \'\',\n'
              '      time: now,\n'
              '      isMe: true,\n'
              '      status: MessageStatus.sent,\n'
              '      isPoll: true,\n'
              '      pollQuestion: result.question,\n'
              '      pollOptions: result.options,\n'
              '      pollVotes: List<int>.filled(result.options.length, 0),\n'
              '    ));\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  /// Composes and sends a split bill. Participants are the chat\'s people — the\n'
              '  /// members of a group, or you and the friend in a 1:1 — with you as the\n'
              '  /// creator who fronted it.\n'
              '  Future<void> _handleSplitBill() async {\n'
              '    final me = AppState.profile.value;\n'
              '    final myDigits = me.phone.replaceAll(RegExp(r\'\\D\'), \'\');\n'
              '    final participants = <BillParticipant>[\n'
              '      BillParticipant(name: \'You\', phone: me.phone, isMe: true),\n'
              '    ];\n'
              '    if (widget.chat.contact.isGroup) {\n'
              '      final seen = {myDigits};\n'
              '      for (final member in widget.chat.members) {\n'
              '        final d = member.phone.replaceAll(RegExp(r\'\\D\'), \'\');\n'
              '        if (d.isEmpty || !seen.add(d)) continue;\n'
              '        participants\n'
              '            .add(BillParticipant(name: member.name, phone: member.phone));\n'
              '      }\n'
              '    } else {\n'
              '      final c = widget.chat.contact;\n'
              '      participants.add(BillParticipant(name: c.name, phone: c.phone));\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    if (participants.length < 2) {\n'
              '      ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(const SnackBar(\n'
