              '          content: Text(\'No one to split a bill with here yet.\')));\n'
              '      return;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    final bill = await showBillSplitSheet(\n'
              '      context,\n'
              '      participants: participants,\n'
              '      myPhone: me.phone,\n'
              '      currency: PaymentService.instance.sendCurrency.value,\n'
              '    );\n'
              '    if (bill == null || !mounted) return;\n'
              '    final now = DateTime.now();\n'
              '    _deliver(Message(\n'
              '      id: \'bill_${now.microsecondsSinceEpoch}\',\n'
              '      text: \'\',\n'
              '      time: now,\n'
              '      isMe: true,\n'
              '      status: MessageStatus.sent,\n'
              '      billSplit: bill,\n'
              '    ));\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  /// Pays this device\'s share of the split bill on [message] to whoever\n'
              '  /// created it — the ordinary P2P send (so the trusted-place check still\n'
              '  /// applies), then marks the share paid and tells everyone.\n'
              '  Future<void> _payBillShare(Message message) async {\n'
              '    final bill = message.billSplit;\n'
              '    if (bill == null) return;\n'
              '    final myPhone = AppState.profile.value.phone;\n'
              '    final mine = bill.shareFor(myPhone);\n'
              '    if (mine == null || mine.paid || mine.cents <= 0) return;\n'
              '\n'
              '    // The creator is who collects. Name them from the group roster if we can,\n'
              '    // else from the bill itself.\n'
              '    final creatorPhone = bill.createdByPhone;\n'
              '    final creatorDigits = creatorPhone.replaceAll(RegExp(r\'\\D\'), \'\');\n'
              '    AppUser? found;\n'
              '    for (final u in [widget.chat.contact, ...widget.chat.members]) {\n'
              '      if (u.phone.replaceAll(RegExp(r\'\\D\'), \'\') == creatorDigits) {\n'
              '        found = u;\n'
              '        break;\n'
              '      }\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    final creatorName = found?.name ??\n'
              '        (bill.shares\n'
              '                .where((s) =>\n'
              '                    s.phone.replaceAll(RegExp(r\'\\D\'), \'\') == creatorDigits)\n'
              '                .isNotEmpty\n'
              '            ? bill.shares\n'
              '                .firstWhere((s) =>\n'
              '                    s.phone.replaceAll(RegExp(r\'\\D\'), \'\') == creatorDigits)\n'
              '                .name\n'
              '            : creatorPhone);\n'
              '    final recipient = found ??\n'
              '        AppUser(\n'
              '          id: creatorPhone,\n'
              '          name: creatorName,\n'
              '          avatarColor: Session.colorForPhone(creatorPhone),\n'
              '          phone: creatorPhone,\n'
              '        );\n'
              '\n'
              '    await _payRecipient(\n'
              '      recipient,\n'
              '      cents: mine.cents,\n'
              '      note: \'Split: ${bill.title}\',\n'
              '      acknowledged: true,\n'
              '      onSettled: (status) {\n'
              '        if (status != \'paid\') return;\n'
              '        _store.markBillSharePaid(_chatId, message.id, myPhone);\n'
              '        for (final phone in _relayPhones()) {\n'
              '          RelayService.instance.sendBillPaid(phone, message.id, myPhone);\n'
              '        }\n'
              '      },\n'
              '    );\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  /// Puts a saved reply in the box — not into the chat.\n'
              '  ///\n'
              '  /// Inserted rather than sent, so a word can be changed first: a canned\n'
              '  /// reply that sends itself is a message nobody read before it left. It\n'
              '  /// appends to whatever is already typed rather than replacing it.\n'
              '  Future<void> _handleQuickReply() async {\n'
              '    final reply = await pickQuickReply(context);\n'
              '    if (reply == null || !mounted) return;\n'
              '    final existing = _store.draftFor(_chatId).trimRight();\n'
              '    _store.setDraft(\n'
              '        _chatId, existing.isEmpty ? reply : \'$existing $reply\');\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  /// "Write a message for me": the user says what they want to say, Okay AI\n'
              '  /// drafts it, and it drops into the composer to edit and send. It NEVER\n'
              '  /// reads the conversation — only the instruction the user types goes to the\n'
              '  /// model — so an encrypted chat\'s contents never leave the device, and the\n'
              '  /// draft is inserted, never auto-sent.\n'
              '  Future<void> _handleAiDraft() async {\n'
              '    // Okay AI is off for name-only accounts (no session to meter it), so the\n'
              '    // in-chat draft is too — same rule as the assistant screen.\n'
              '    if (Session.instance.isNumberless) {\n'
              '      ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(const SnackBar(\n'
              '          content: Text(\'Okay AI needs a phone number. Add one to use it.\')));\n'
              '      return;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    final instruction = await showAppTextPrompt(\n'
              '      context,\n'
              '      icon: Icons.auto_awesome,\n'
              '      title: \'Write a message\',\n'
              '      hint: \'What do you want to say? e.g. politely decline the invite\',\n'
              '      maxLines: 3,\n'
              '      capitalization: TextCapitalization.sentences,\n'
              '    );\n'
              '    if (instruction == null || instruction.trim().isEmpty || !mounted) return;\n'
              '    final messenger = ScaffoldMessenger.of(context);\n'
              '    messenger.showSnackBar(const SnackBar(\n'
              '        content: Text(\'Drafting…\'), duration: Duration(milliseconds: 900)));\n'
              '    final draft = await AiAssistant.instance.draft(instruction);\n'
              '    if (!mounted) return;\n'
              '    if (draft == null) {\n'
              '      messenger.showSnackBar(const SnackBar(\n'
              '          content: Text(\'Couldn\\\'t draft that. Is the assistant set up?\')));\n'
              '      return;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    final existing = _store.draftFor(_chatId).trimRight();\n'
              '    _store.setDraft(_chatId, existing.isEmpty ? draft : \'$existing $draft\');\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  /// Builds a form and sends it as a message.\n'
              '  Future<void> _handleCreateForm() async {\n'
              '    final result =\n'
              '        await Navigator.of(context).push<(String, List<FormFieldSpec>)>(\n'
              '      MaterialPageRoute(builder: (_) => const FormBuilderScreen()),\n'
              '    );\n'
              '    if (result == null || !mounted) return;\n'
              '    final now = DateTime.now();\n'
              '    _deliver(Message(\n'
              '      id: \'form_${now.microsecondsSinceEpoch}\',\n'
              '      text: \'\',\n'
              '      time: now,\n'
              '      isMe: true,\n'
              '      status: MessageStatus.sent,\n'
              '      isForm: true,\n'
              '      formTitle: result.$1,\n'
              '      formFields: result.$2,\n'
              '    ));\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  /// Fills in somebody\'s form and sends the answers back.\n'
              '  Future<void> _handleFillForm(Message message) async {\n'
              '    final mine = Session.instance.user.value?.name ?? \'\';\n'
              '    final previous = message.formResponses\n'
              '        .where((r) => r.from == mine)\n'
              '        .map((r) => r.answers)\n'
              '        .toList();\n'
              '    final answers = await Navigator.of(context).push<List<String>>(\n'
              '      MaterialPageRoute(\n'
              '        builder: (_) => FormFillScreen(\n'
              '          title: message.formTitle,\n'
              '          fields: message.formFields,\n'
              '          initial: previous.isEmpty ? const [] : previous.first,\n'
              '        ),\n'
              '      ),\n'
              '    );\n'
              '    if (answers == null || !mounted) return;\n'
              '    // Recorded here too, so the sender sees their own answers in the same\n'
              '    // list as everybody\'s rather than only the people who replied to them.\n'
              '    _store.applyFormResponse(_chatId, message.id,\n'
              '        FormResponse(from: mine, answers: answers, at: DateTime.now()));\n'
              '    // The answers go to the form\'s AUTHOR alone. In a group that is the\n'
              '    // member who sent it — senderPhone rides every group message — and\n'
              '    // deliberately not the whole room: recipients are never shown who else\n'
              '    // answered, so their devices have no business holding the answers\n'
              '    // either. This used to be gated on the chat\'s CONTACT being a real\n'
              '    // peer, which a group\'s pseudo-contact never is — so a group form\'s\n'
              '    // answers went nowhere at all.\n'
              '    final author = widget.chat.contact.isGroup\n'
              '        ? message.senderPhone\n'
              '        : (_isRealPeer(widget.chat.contact)\n'
              '            ? widget.chat.contact.phone\n'
              '            : \'\');\n'
              '    if (RelayConfig.isEnabled &&\n'
              '        RelayService.digits(author).isNotEmpty) {\n'
              '      RelayService.instance.sendFormResponse(author, message.id, answers);\n'
              '    }\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  /// Shows what came back.\n'
              '  void _openFormResponses(Message message) {\n'
              '    Navigator.of(context).push(MaterialPageRoute(\n'
              '      builder: (_) => FormResponsesScreen(\n'
              '        title: message.formTitle,\n'
              '        fields: message.formFields,\n'
              '        responses: message.formResponses,\n'
              '      ),\n'
              '    ));\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  /// Records the local vote and syncs it to everyone the chat reaches.\n'
              '  void _handleVotePoll(Message message, int option) {\n'
              '    final previous = _store.votePoll(_chatId, message.id, option);\n'
              '    if (previous == option) return; // no change\n'
              '    for (final phone in _relayPhones()) {\n'
              '      RelayService.instance\n'
              '          .sendPollVote(phone, message.id, option, previous);\n'
              '    }\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  /// Sends a sticker: an emoji drawn huge, or one of the user\'s own photos\n'
              '  /// — both riding the same sealed paths words and photos already use.\n'
              '  Future<void> _handleSendSticker() async {\n'
              '    final choice = await showStickerSheet(context);\n'
              '    if (choice == null || !mounted) return;\n'
              '    String? photoUri = choice.photoUri;\n'
              '    if (choice.newPhoto) {\n'
              '      try {\n'
              '        photoUri = await PhotoPrep.pickPhoto();\n'
              '      } on FileRejected catch (e) {\n'
              '        if (mounted) {\n'
              '          ScaffoldMessenger.of(context)\n'
              '              .showSnackBar(SnackBar(content: Text(e.reason)));\n'
              '        }\n'
              '        return;\n'
              '      }\n'
              '      if (photoUri == null || !mounted) return;\n'
              '      // Saved on send, so reusing it never means re-picking it.\n'
              '      StickerStore.instance.savePhoto(photoUri);\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    if (!mounted) return;\n'
              '    final now = DateTime.now();\n'
              '    if (photoUri != null) {\n'
              '      _deliver(Message(\n'
              '        id: \'stk_${now.microsecondsSinceEpoch}\',\n'
              '        text: \'\',\n'
              '        time: now,\n'
              '        isMe: true,\n'
              '        status: MessageStatus.sent,\n'
              '        isSticker: true,\n'
              '        isImage: true,\n'
              '        imageUrl: photoUri,\n'
              '      ));\n'
              '      return;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    final emoji = choice.emoji;\n'
              '    if (emoji == null) return;\n'
              '    StickerStore.instance.noteUsed(emoji);\n'
              '    _deliver(Message(\n'
              '      id: \'stk_${now.microsecondsSinceEpoch}\',\n'
              '      text: emoji,\n'
              '      time: now,\n'
              '      isMe: true,\n'
              '      status: MessageStatus.sent,\n'
              '      isSticker: true,\n'
              '    ));\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  /// GIFs moved off the composer bar and into the attachment panel — the\n'
              '  /// bar was drowning in buttons.\n'
              '  Future<void> _pickGifAttachment() async {\n'
              '    final picked = await showEmojiGifSheet(context, initialTab: 1);\n'
              '    final url = picked?.gif?.url;\n'
              '    if (url != null) _handleSendGif(url);\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  /// The composer\'s attachment options, shown inline above the keyboard.\n'
              '  List<AttachmentOption> _attachmentOptions() => [\n'
              '        AttachmentOption(\n'
              '            icon: Icons.photo,\n'
              '            label: \'Photos\',\n'
              '            color: const Color(0xFFC861F9),\n'
              '            onTap: _handleSendImage),\n'
              '        AttachmentOption(\n'
              '            icon: Icons.gif_box_outlined,\n'
              '            label: \'GIF\',\n'
              '            color: const Color(0xFFF97052),\n'
              '            onTap: _pickGifAttachment),\n'
              '        AttachmentOption(\n'
              '            icon: Icons.emoji_emotions_outlined,\n'
              '            label: \'Sticker\',\n'
              '            color: const Color(0xFFFF5C8A),\n'
              '            onTap: _handleSendSticker),\n'
              '        AttachmentOption(\n'
              '            icon: Icons.timer_outlined,\n'
              '            label: \'View once\',\n'
              '            color: const Color(0xFF0A84FF),\n'
              '            onTap: () => _handleSendImage(viewOnce: true)),\n'
              '        AttachmentOption(\n'
              '            icon: Icons.blur_on,\n'
              '            label: \'Ghost message\',\n'
              '            color: const Color(0xFF5E5CE6),\n'
              '            onTap: _composeGhost),\n'
              '        AttachmentOption(\n'
              '            icon: Icons.insert_drive_file,\n'
              '            label: \'Document\',\n'
              '            color: const Color(0xFF7F66FF),\n'
              '            onTap: _handleSendDocument),\n'
              '        AttachmentOption(\n'
              '            icon: Icons.location_on,\n'
              '            label: \'Location\',\n'
              '            color: const Color(0xFF1FA855),\n'
              '            onTap: _handleSendLocation),\n'
              '        AttachmentOption(\n'
              '            icon: Icons.person,\n'
              '            label: \'Contact\',\n'
              '            color: const Color(0xFF009DE2),\n'
              '            onTap: _pickContactToShare),\n'
              '\n'
              '        // Not offered in your own notes: there is no second party to pay.\n'
              '        // Groups keep it — the option asks which member first.\n'
              '        if (!_isNoteToSelf)\n'
              '          AttachmentOption(\n'
              '              icon: Icons.attach_money,\n'
              '              label: \'Payment\',\n'
              '              color: const Color(0xFF12B76A),\n'
              '              onTap: _handleSendMoney),\n'
              '        // Asking, not sending — 1:1 only, because a group request has no\n'
              '        // single payer to answer it.\n'
              '        if (!_isNoteToSelf && !widget.chat.contact.isGroup)\n'
              '          AttachmentOption(\n'
              '              icon: Icons.request_quote_outlined,\n'
              '              label: \'Request\',\n'
              '              color: const Color(0xFF5B6BF0),\n'
              '              onTap: _requestMoney),\n'
              '        // Split a bill — a group OR a 1:1, but never your own notes (nobody\n'
              '        // to split with).\n'
              '        if (!_isNoteToSelf)\n'
              '          AttachmentOption(\n'
              '              icon: Icons.receipt_long_outlined,\n'
              '              label: \'Split bill\',\n'
              '              color: const Color(0xFF0E9F6E),\n'
              '              onTap: _handleSplitBill),\n'
              '        // A wordless "hey" — 1:1 only: poking a whole group is a fire\n'
              '        // alarm, and there is nobody to poke in your own notes.\n'
              '        if (!_isNoteToSelf && !widget.chat.contact.isGroup)\n'
              '          AttachmentOption(\n'
              '              icon: Icons.waving_hand_outlined,\n'
              '              label: \'Poke\',\n'
              '              color: const Color(0xFFE8A33D),\n'
              '              onTap: _handlePoke),\n'
              '        AttachmentOption(\n'
              '            icon: Icons.poll_outlined,\n'
              '            label: \'Poll\',\n'
              '            color: const Color(0xFF7F66FF),\n'
              '            onTap: _handleCreatePoll),\n'
              '        AttachmentOption(\n'
              '            icon: Icons.bolt_outlined,\n'
              '            label: \'Quick reply\',\n'
              '            color: const Color(0xFFF79009),\n'
              '            onTap: _handleQuickReply),\n'
              '        AttachmentOption(\n'
              '            icon: Icons.assignment_outlined,\n'
              '            label: \'Form\',\n'
              '            color: const Color(0xFF2E90FA),\n'
              '            onTap: _handleCreateForm),\n'
              '        AttachmentOption(\n'
              '            icon: Icons.auto_awesome,\n'
              '            label: \'AI draft\',\n'
              '            color: const Color(0xFF17708A),\n'
              '            onTap: _handleAiDraft),\n'
              '      ];\n'
              '\n'
              '  @override\n'
              '  Widget build(BuildContext context) {\n'
              '    final isDark = Theme.of(context).brightness == Brightness.dark;\n'
              '    final contact = widget.chat.contact;\n'
              '\n'
              '    return ListenableBuilder(\n'
              '      // Rebuild on wallpaper and the Okay Pro custom bubble color, and on\n'
              '      // the chat store — so mute / disappearing / pin toggled from the\n'
              '      // contact-info screen refresh the header the moment you return.\n'
              '      //\n'
              '      // The streak store too: a streak advances, lapses, or gets agreed with\n'
              '      // the peer (reconcile, straight off the relay) without the chat store\n'
              '      // changing at all, and the header\'s flame has to follow it rather than\n'
              '      // waiting for the next unrelated redraw.\n'
              '      listenable: Listenable.merge([\n'
              '        AppState.chatWallpaper,\n'
              '        AppState.bubbleColor,\n'
              '        _store,\n'
              '        StreakStore.instance,\n'
              '      ]),\n'
              '      builder: (context, _) {\n'
              '        final globalWallpaper = AppState.chatWallpaper.value;\n'
              '        return Scaffold(\n'
              '        // A per-chat wallpaper overrides the global default.\n'
              '        backgroundColor: (_store.wallpaperFor(_chatId) ?? globalWallpaper) ??\n'
              '            (isDark ? AppColors.chatBgDark : AppColors.chatBgLight),\n'
              '        appBar: _selectionMode\n'
              '            ? AppBar(\n'
              '                leading: IconButton(\n'
              '                  icon: const Icon(Icons.close),\n'
              '                  onPressed: _exitSelection,\n'
              '                ),\n'
              '                title: Text(\'${_selectedIds.length}\'),\n'
              '                actions: [\n'
              '                  IconButton(\n'
              '                    icon: const Icon(Icons.star_border),\n'
              '                    tooltip: \'Star\',\n'
              '                    onPressed: _starSelected,\n'
              '                  ),\n'
              '                  IconButton(\n'
              '                    icon: const Icon(Icons.delete_outline),\n'
              '                    tooltip: \'Delete\',\n'
              '                    onPressed: _deleteSelected,\n'
              '                  ),\n'
              '                  if (_selectionMayLeave) ...[\n'
              '                    IconButton(\n'
              '                      icon: const Icon(Icons.shortcut),\n'
              '                      tooltip: \'Forward\',\n'
              '                      onPressed: _forwardSelected,\n'
              '                    ),\n'
              '                    if (_selectedIds.length == 1)\n'
              '                      IconButton(\n'
              '                        icon: const Icon(Icons.copy),\n'
              '                        tooltip: \'Copy\',\n'
              '                        onPressed: _copySelected,\n'
              '                      ),\n'
              '                  ] else\n'
              '                    // Absent rather than disabled: a greyed-out Forward is a\n'
              '                    // button somebody taps twice before believing it.\n'
              '                    IconButton(\n'
              '                      icon: const Icon(Icons.lock_outline),\n'
              '                      tooltip: \'Protected — this cannot be forwarded or copied\',\n'
              '                      onPressed: () => ScaffoldMessenger.of(context)\n'
              '                          .showSnackBar(const SnackBar(\n'
              '                              content: Text(\'This conversation is protected. \'\n'
              '                                  \'Messages in it cannot be forwarded or \'\n'
              '                                  \'copied.\'))),\n'
              '                    ),\n'
              '                ],\n'
              '              )\n'
              '            : _searching\n'
              '                ? AppBar(\n'
              '                    leading: IconButton(\n'
              '                      icon: const Icon(Icons.arrow_back),\n'
              '                      onPressed: _exitSearch,\n'
              '                    ),\n'
              '                    titleSpacing: 0,\n'
              '                    title: TextField(\n'
              '                      controller: _searchController,\n'
              '                      autofocus: true,\n'
              '                      decoration: const InputDecoration(\n'
              '                        hintText: \'Search this chat\',\n'
              '                        border: InputBorder.none,\n'
              '                      ),\n'
              '                      onChanged: (v) => setState(() => _searchQuery = v),\n'
              '                    ),\n'
              '                    actions: [\n'
              '                      if (_searchQuery.trim().isNotEmpty)\n'
              '                        Center(\n'
              '                          child: Padding(\n'
              '                            padding: const EdgeInsets.only(right: 4),\n'
              '                            child: Text(\n'
              '                              _visibleMessages.isEmpty\n'
              '                                  ? \'No matches\'\n'
              '                                  : \'${_visibleMessages.length} found\',\n'
              '                              style: TextStyle(\n'
              '                                  color: AppColors.subtle(context), fontSize: 13),\n'
              '                            ),\n'
              '                          ),\n'
              '                        ),\n'
              '                      if (_searchQuery.isNotEmpty)\n'
              '                        IconButton(\n'
              '                          icon: const Icon(Icons.close),\n'
              '                          onPressed: () => setState(() {\n'
              '                            _searchQuery = \'\';\n'
              '                            _searchController.clear();\n'
              '                          }),\n'
              '                        ),\n'
              '                    ],\n'
              '                  )\n'
              '                : _inThread\n'
              '                    // A thread is a different room to be in, and the header\n'
              '                    // has to say so — the same name and avatar as the group\n'
              '                    // would leave somebody typing into a side conversation\n'
              '                    // believing they were talking to everyone.\n'
              '                    ? AppBar(\n'
              '                        title: Column(\n'
              '                          crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,\n'
              '                          mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,\n'
              '                          children: [\n'
              '                            const Text(\'Thread\',\n'
              '                                style: TextStyle(\n'
              '                                    fontSize: 17,\n'
              '                                    fontWeight: FontWeight.w600)),\n'
              '                            Text(\n'
              '                              \'Stays out of ${contact.name}\',\n'
              '                              style: TextStyle(\n'
              '                                  fontSize: 12.5,\n'
              '                                  fontWeight: FontWeight.normal,\n'
              '                                  color: AppColors.subtle(context)),\n'
              '                            ),\n'
              '                          ],\n'
              '                        ),\n'
              '                      )\n'
              '                    : AppBar(\n'
              '                    titleSpacing: 0,\n'
              '                    title: InkWell(\n'
              '                      onTap: () => Navigator.of(context).push(\n'
              '                        MaterialPageRoute(\n'
              '                          builder: (_) => contact.isGroup\n'
              '                              ? GroupInfoScreen(group: contact, members: widget.chat.members, chatId: _chatId)\n'
              '                              : ContactInfoScreen(user: contact, chatId: _chatId),\n'
              '                        ),\n'
              '                      ),\n'
              '                      child: Row(\n'
              '                        children: [\n'
              '                          UserAvatar(\n'
              '                            user: contact,\n'
              '                            radius: 18,\n'
              '                            heroTag: \'chatHeaderAvatar\',\n'
              '                          ),\n'
              '                          const SizedBox(width: 10),\n'
              '                          Expanded(\n'
              '                            child: Column(\n'
              '                              crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,\n'
              '                              mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.center,\n'
              '                              children: [\n'
              '                                Row(\n'
              '                                  children: [\n'
              '                                    Flexible(\n'
              '                                      child: NameWithBadge(\n'
              '                                        // Bare numbers print like a phone\n'
              '                                        // would show them.\n'
              '                                        name: formatPhoneForDisplay(\n'
              '                                            contact.name),\n'
              '                                        verified: contact.verified,\n'
              '                                        business: contact.isBusiness,\n'
              '                                        badgeSize: 16,\n'
              '                                        style: const TextStyle(\n'
              '                                          fontSize: 17,\n'
              '                                          fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,\n'
              '                                        ),\n'
              '                                        trailing: () {\n'
              '                                          final s = StreakStore.instance\n'
              '                                              .streakFor(_chatId);\n'
              '                                          return s > 0\n'
              '                                              ? StreakChip(\n'
              '                                                  count: s,\n'
              '                                                  expiring: StreakStore.instance\n'
              '                                                      .isExpiringSoon(_chatId),\n'
              '                                                )\n'
              '                                              : null;\n'
              '                                        }(),\n'
              '                                      ),\n'
              '                                    ),\n'
              '                                    if (_store.chatById(_chatId)?.isMuted ??\n'
              '                                        false) ...[\n'
              '                                      const SizedBox(width: 6),\n'
              '                                      Icon(\n'
              '                                        Icons.volume_off,\n'
              '                                        size: 16,\n'
              '                                        color: isDark\n'
              '                                            ? Colors.white54\n'
              '                                            : Colors.black45,\n'
              '                                      ),\n'
              '                                    ],\n'
              '                                    if (_store\n'
              '                                            .chatById(_chatId)\n'
              '                                            ?.confirmBeforeSend ??\n'
              '                                        false) ...[\n'
              '                                      const SizedBox(width: 6),\n'
              '                                      Icon(\n'
              '                                        Icons.verified_user,\n'
              '                                        size: 15,\n'
              '                                        color: AppColors.accentOn(context),\n'
              '                                      ),\n'
              '                                    ],\n'
              '                                  ],\n'
              '                                ),\n'
              '                                _isTyping\n'
              '                                    ? Row(\n'
              '                                        mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,\n'
              '                                        children: [\n'
              '                                          if (_typingName.isNotEmpty)\n'
              '                                            Padding(\n'
              '                                              padding: const EdgeInsets.only(\n'
              '                                                  right: 4),\n'
              '                                              child: Text(\n'
              '                                                _typingName,\n'
              '                                                style: TextStyle(\n'
              '                                                  fontSize: 12.5,\n'
              '                                                  color: AppColors.accentOn(\n'
              '                                                      context),\n'
              '                                                ),\n'
              '                                              ),\n'
              '                                            ),\n'
              '                                          TypingIndicator(\n'
              '                                            color: AppColors.accentOn(context),\n'
              '                                          ),\n'
              '                                        ],\n'
              '                                      )\n'
              '                                    : Text(\n'
              '                                        () {\n'
              '                                          // Three honest rungs: their ping\n'
              '                                          // said they are in THIS chat, or\n'
              '                                          // the app answered from elsewhere\n'
              '                                          // (online), or nothing fresh.\n'
              '                                          final presence = _peerOnline &&\n'
              '                                                  _peerWhere == \'chat\'\n'
              '                                              ? \'in this chat\'\n'
              '                                              : (contact.isOnline ||\n'
              '                                                      _peerOnline)\n'
              '                                                  ? \'online\'\n'
              '                                                  : \'last seen recently\';\n'
              '                                          // A business says what it is where\n'
              '                                          // you\'re actually talking to it.\n'
              '                                          if (!contact.isBusiness) {\n'
              '                                            return presence;\n'
              '                                          }\n'
              '                                          final label = contact\n'
              '                                                  .businessCategory\n'
              '                                                  .trim()\n'
              '                                                  .isEmpty\n'
              '                                              ? \'Business\'\n'
              '                                              : contact.businessCategory\n'
              '                                                  .trim();\n'
              '                                          return \'$label · $presence\';\n'
              '                                        }(),\n'
              '                                        style: TextStyle(\n'
              '                                          fontSize: 12.5,\n'
              '                                          color: isDark\n'
              '                                              ? Colors.white70\n'
              '                                              : Colors.black54,\n'
              '                                        ),\n'
              '                                      ),\n'
              '                              ],\n'
              '                            ),\n'
              '                          ),\n'
              '                        ],\n'
              '                      ),\n'
              '                    ),\n'
              '                    actions: [\n'
              '                      if ((_store.chatById(_chatId)?.disappearingSeconds ?? 0) >\n'
              '                          0)\n'
              '                        IconButton(\n'
              '                          icon: const Icon(Icons.timer_outlined),\n'
              '                          tooltip: \'Disappearing messages on\',\n'
              '                          onPressed: _chooseDisappearing,\n'
              '                        ),\n'
              '                      // Your own notes have nobody on the other end to ring.\n'
              '                      if (!_isNoteToSelf) ...[\n'
              '                        IconButton(\n'
              '                          icon: const Icon(Icons.call),\n'
              '                          onPressed: () => _startCall(video: false),\n'
              '                        ),\n'
              '                        IconButton(\n'
              '                          icon: const Icon(Icons.videocam),\n'
              '                          onPressed: () => _startCall(video: true),\n'
              '                        ),\n'
              '                      ],\n'
              '                      // The overflow menu is gone from this bar. Of the four\n'
              '                      // things it held, two were already reachable — tapping\n'
              '                      // the name opens Contact & chat settings, which is\n'
              '                      // where "Media, links, and docs" lives — and "Send as\n'
              '                      // text (SMS)" moved there too, being a thing you decide\n'
              '                      // about a person rather than about this moment.\n'
              '                      //\n'
              '                      // Search stays here, as an icon. It is a MODE OF THIS\n'
              '                      // SCREEN, not a setting: searching a conversation is\n'
              '                      // something you do while reading it, and routing it\n'
              '                      // through a settings screen would mean leaving the\n'
              '                      // thing you are searching.\n'
              '                      IconButton(\n'
              '                        icon: const Icon(Icons.search),\n'
              '                        tooltip: \'Search this chat\',\n'
              '                        onPressed: () => setState(() => _searching = true),\n'
              '                      ),\n'
              '                      // The pinboard is the other mode of this screen: the\n'
              '                      // same conversation with the scrolling taken out —\n'
              '                      // pins, links, photos, places and files in one place.\n'
              '                      IconButton(\n'
              '                        icon: const Icon(Icons.push_pin_outlined),\n'
              '                        tooltip: \'Pinboard\',\n'
              '                        onPressed: () => Navigator.of(context).push(\n'
              '                          MaterialPageRoute(\n'
              '                            builder: (_) => MediaGalleryScreen(\n'
              '                              chatId: _chatId,\n'
              '                              contactName: contact.name,\n'
              '                            ),\n'
              '                          ),\n'
              '                        ),\n'
              '                      ),\n'
              '                    ],\n'
              '                  ),\n'
              '        // A screenshot can only be announced after the fact. A recording is\n'
              '        // still going, so the honest thing is to take the conversation off\n'
              '        // the screen for as long as it lasts — announcing and carrying on\n'
              '        // would be telling somebody their messages are being filmed while\n'
              '        // filming them.\n'
              '        body: _store.isProtected(_chatId) &&\n'
              '                ScreenshotWatch.instance.capturing.value\n'
              '            ? _capturedNotice(context)\n'
              '            : Column(\n'
              '          children: [\n'
              '            ListenableBuilder(\n'
              '              listenable: _store,\n'
              '              builder: (context, _) {\n'
              '                final chat = _store.chatById(_chatId);\n'
              '                final pinnedId = chat?.pinnedMessageId;\n'
              '                if (pinnedId == null) return const SizedBox.shrink();\n'
              '                final matches = chat!.messages.where((m) => m.id == pinnedId);\n'
              '                if (matches.isEmpty) return const SizedBox.shrink();\n'
              '                final count = chat.pinnedMessageIds.length;\n'
              '                return _PinnedBanner(\n'
              '                  message: matches.first,\n'
              '                  count: count,\n'
              '                  onTap: count > 1 ? _showPinnedSheet : null,\n'
              '                  onUnpin: () => _store.unpinMessage(_chatId, pinnedId),\n'
              '                );\n'
              '              },\n'
              '            ),\n'
              '            Expanded(\n'
              '              child: Stack(\n'
              '                children: [\n'
              '                  ListenableBuilder(\n'
              '                    listenable: _store,\n'
              '                    builder: (context, _) {\n'
              '                      final items = _buildItems();\n'
              '                      if (items.isEmpty && !_searching) {\n'
              '                        return RefreshIndicator(\n'
              '                          onRefresh: _refreshChat,\n'
              '                          child: LayoutBuilder(\n'
              '                            builder: (context, constraints) =>\n'
              '                                SingleChildScrollView(\n'
              '                              // Scrollable even when empty, or there is\n'
              '                              // nothing to pull.\n'
              '                              physics: const AlwaysScrollableScrollPhysics(),\n'
              '                              child: SizedBox(\n'
              '                                height: constraints.maxHeight,\n'
              '                                child: const _EmptyConversation(),\n'
              '                              ),\n'
              '                            ),\n'
              '                          ),\n'
              '                        );\n'
              '                      }\n'
              '                      return RefreshIndicator(\n'
              '                        onRefresh: _refreshChat,\n'
              '                        child: ListView(\n'
              '                          controller: _scrollController,\n'
              '                          // Drag the transcript at all and the keyboard goes\n'
              '                          // away — the iOS-native "swipe to dismiss" gesture,\n'
              '                          // and the answer to "there\'s no way to put the\n'
              '                          // keyboard away". A wrapping tap handler was tried\n'
              '                          // and fought the message double-tap, so the drag\n'
              '                          // gesture stands alone.\n'
              '                          keyboardDismissBehavior:\n'
              '                              ScrollViewKeyboardDismissBehavior.onDrag,\n'
              '                          // Short transcripts don\'t fill the screen and would\n'
              '                          // otherwise have no overscroll to pull against.\n'
              '                          physics: const AlwaysScrollableScrollPhysics(),\n'
              '                          padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 8),\n'
              '                          children: items,\n'
              '                        ),\n'
              '                      );\n'
              '                    },\n'
              '                  ),\n'
              '                  if (_showScrollToBottom)\n'
              '                    Positioned(\n'
              '                      right: 12,\n'
              '                      bottom: 12,\n'
              '                      child: FloatingActionButton.small(\n'
              '                        heroTag: \'scrollToBottom\',\n'
              '                        backgroundColor:\n'
              '                            isDark ? AppColors.darkAppBar : Colors.white,\n'
              '                        // Theme-aware: the light accent is invisible on the\n'
              '                        // dark app-bar surface.\n'
              '                        foregroundColor: isDark\n'
              '                            ? Colors.white\n'
              '                            : AppColors.accentOn(context),\n'
              '                        elevation: 2,\n'
              '                        onPressed: _animateToBottom,\n'
              '                        child: const Icon(Icons.keyboard_arrow_down),\n'
              '                      ),\n'
              '                    ),\n'
              '                ],\n'
              '              ),\n'
              '            ),\n'
              '            ListenableBuilder(\n'
              '              listenable: Scheduler.instance,\n'
              '              builder: (context, _) {\n'
              '                final count = Scheduler.instance.pendingFor(_chatId).length;\n'
              '                if (count == 0) return const SizedBox.shrink();\n'
              '                return Material(\n'
              '                  color: AppColors.accentOn(context).withValues(alpha: 0.12),\n'
              '                  child: InkWell(\n'
              '                    onTap: _showScheduledSheet,\n'
              '                    child: Padding(\n'
              '                      padding:\n'
              '                          const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 16, vertical: 10),\n'
              '                      child: Row(\n'
              '                        children: [\n'
              '                          Icon(Icons.schedule,\n'
              '                              size: 18, color: AppColors.accentOn(context)),\n'
              '                          const SizedBox(width: 10),\n'
              '                          Expanded(\n'
              '                            child: Text(\n'
              '                              count == 1\n'
              '                                  ? \'1 message scheduled\'\n'
              '                                  : \'$count messages scheduled\',\n'
              '                              style: TextStyle(\n'
              '                                color: AppColors.accentOn(context),\n'
              '                                fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,\n'
              '                                fontSize: 13.5,\n'
              '                              ),\n'
              '                            ),\n'
              '                          ),\n'
              '                          Icon(Icons.chevron_right,\n'
              '                              size: 20, color: AppColors.accentOn(context)),\n'
              '                        ],\n'
              '                      ),\n'
              '                    ),\n'
              '                  ),\n'
              '                );\n'
              '              },\n'
              '            ),\n'
              '            // A request explains itself where the reply would happen:\n'
              '            // reading is free, replying accepts, blocking is one tap.\n'
              '            if (!_selectionMode)\n'
              '              ListenableBuilder(\n'
              '                listenable: _store,\n'
              '                builder: (context, _) {\n'
              '                  if (!(_store.chatById(_chatId)?.isRequest ?? false)) {\n'
              '                    return const SizedBox.shrink();\n'
              '                  }\n'
              '                  return _RequestBanner(\n'
              '                    name: widget.chat.contact.name,\n'
              '                    onAccept: () => _store.acceptRequest(_chatId),\n'
              '                    onBlock: () {\n'
              '                      AppState.setBlocked(widget.chat.contact.phone, true);\n'
              '                      _store.deleteChat(_chatId);\n'
              '                      Navigator.of(context).pop();\n'
              '                    },\n'
              '                  );\n'
              '                },\n'
              '              ),\n'
              '            if (!_selectionMode)\n'
              '              ValueListenableBuilder<Set<String>>(\n'
              '                valueListenable: AppState.blockedContacts,\n'
              '                builder: (context, _, __) {\n'
              '                  if (AppState.isBlocked(widget.chat.contact.phone)) {\n'
              '                    return _BlockedBanner(\n'
              '                      name: widget.chat.contact.name,\n'
              '                      onUnblock: () => AppState.setBlocked(\n'
              '                          widget.chat.contact.phone, false),\n'
              '                    );\n'
              '                  }\n'
              '                  return ChatInputBar(\n'
              '                    onSend: _handleSend,\n'
              '                    onSendGif: _handleSendGif,\n'
              '                    attachments: _attachmentOptions(),\n'
              '                    onSendVoice: _handleSendVoice,\n'
              '                    onTyping: _onTyping,\n'
              '                    onSchedule: _scheduleMessage,\n'
              '                    replyTo: _replyTo,\n'
              '                    onCancelReply: () => setState(() => _replyTo = null),\n'
              '                    initialText: _store.draftFor(_chatId),\n'
              '                    onChanged: (t) => _store.setDraft(_chatId, t),\n'
              '                    confirmSend: _confirmRecipient,\n'
              '                    mentionNames: _mentionNames(),\n'
              '                    suggestedReplies: _suggested,\n'
              '                  );\n'
              '                },\n'
              '              ),\n'
              '          ],\n'
              '        ),\n'
              '        );\n'
              '      },\n'
              '    );\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  void _showComingSoon(BuildContext context, String feature) {\n'
              '    ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(\n'
              '      SnackBar(content: Text(\'$feature is not available in this demo\')),\n'
              '    );\n'
              '  }\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'

              'class _PinnedBanner extends StatelessWidget {\n'
              '  final Message message;\n'
              '  final int count;\n'
              '  final VoidCallback? onTap;\n'
              '  final VoidCallback onUnpin;\n'
              '\n'
              '  const _PinnedBanner({\n'
              '    required this.message,\n'
              '    required this.count,\n'
              '    required this.onUnpin,\n'
              '    this.onTap,\n'
              '  });\n'
              '\n'
              '  @override\n'
              '  Widget build(BuildContext context) {\n'
              '    final isDark = Theme.of(context).brightness == Brightness.dark;\n'
              '    return Material(\n'
              '      color: isDark ? AppColors.darkAppBar : Colors.white,\n'
              '      child: InkWell(\n'
              '        onTap: onTap,\n'
              '        child: Container(\n'
              '          padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(12, 8, 8, 8),\n'
              '          decoration: BoxDecoration(\n'
              '            border: Border(\n'
              '              left: BorderSide(color: AppColors.accentOn(context), width: 4),\n'
              '            ),\n'
              '          ),\n'
              '          child: Row(\n'
              '            children: [\n'
              '              const Icon(Icons.push_pin, size: 16, color: Colors.grey),\n'
              '              const SizedBox(width: 10),\n'
              '              Expanded(\n'
              '                child: Column(\n'
              '                  crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,\n'
              '                  mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,\n'
              '                  children: [\n'
              '                    Text(\n'
              '                      count > 1 ? \'$count pinned messages\' : \'Pinned message\',\n'
              '                      style: TextStyle(\n'
              '                        fontSize: 12,\n'
              '                        fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,\n'
              '                        color: AppColors.accentOn(context),\n'
              '                      ),\n'
              '                    ),\n'
              '                    Text(\n'
              '                      message.isVoice\n'
              '                          ? \'Voice message\'\n'
              '                          : message.isImage\n'
              '                              ? \'Photo\'\n'
              '                              : message.text,\n'
              '                      maxLines: 1,\n'
              '                      overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,\n'
              '                      style: TextStyle(\n'
              '                        fontSize: 13.5,\n'
              '                        color: isDark ? Colors.white70 : Colors.black54,\n'
              '                      ),\n'
              '                    ),\n'
              '                  ],\n'
              '                ),\n'
              '              ),\n'
              '              IconButton(\n'
              '                icon: const Icon(Icons.close, size: 20),\n'
              '                color: Colors.grey,\n'
              '                onPressed: onUnpin,\n'
              '                tooltip: \'Unpin\',\n'
              '              ),\n'
              '            ],\n'
              '          ),\n'
              '        ),\n'
              '      ),\n'
              '    );\n'
              '  }\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              '/// Fades and gently slides a message into view once, when it first appears.\n'
              'class _MessageEntrance extends StatefulWidget {\n'
              '  final Widget child;\n'
              '\n'
              '  const _MessageEntrance({super.key, required this.child});\n'
              '\n'
              '  @override\n'
              '  State<_MessageEntrance> createState() => _MessageEntranceState();\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              'class _MessageEntranceState extends State<_MessageEntrance>\n'
              '    with SingleTickerProviderStateMixin {\n'
              '  late final AnimationController _controller = AnimationController(\n'
              '    vsync: this,\n'
              '    duration: const Duration(milliseconds: 220),\n'
              '  )..forward();\n'
              '\n'
              '  late final Animation<double> _curve =\n'
              '      CurvedAnimation(parent: _controller, curve: Curves.easeOut);\n'
              '\n'
              '  @override\n'
              '  void dispose() {\n'
              '    _controller.dispose();\n'
              '    super.dispose();\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  @override\n'
              '  Widget build(BuildContext context) {\n'
              '    return FadeTransition(\n'
              '      opacity: _curve,\n'
              '      child: SlideTransition(\n'
              '        position: Tween<Offset>(\n'
              '          begin: const Offset(0, 0.06),\n'
              '          end: Offset.zero,\n'
              '        ).animate(_curve),\n'
              '        child: widget.child,\n'
              '      ),\n'
              '    );\n'
              '  }\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              '/// A full-width "N unread messages" separator, shown above the first message\n'
              '/// that was unread when the chat was opened.\n'
              'class _UnreadDivider extends StatelessWidget {\n'
              '  final int count;\n'
              '\n'
              '  const _UnreadDivider({required this.count});\n'
              '\n'
              '  @override\n'
              '  Widget build(BuildContext context) {\n'
              '    final isDark = Theme.of(context).brightness == Brightness.dark;\n'
              '    // Near-white accent in dark mode so the label is readable on the soft\n'
              '    // dark background (the mono ink is near-black and would vanish).\n'
              '    final accent = isDark ? const Color(0xFFB9C1C9) : AppColors.accentOn(context);\n'
              '    return Center(\n'
              '      child: Container(\n'
              '        margin: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 8),\n'
              '        padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 14, vertical: 5),\n'
              '        decoration: BoxDecoration(\n'
              '          color: (isDark ? Colors.white : AppColors.accentOn(context))\n'
              '              .withValues(alpha: isDark ? 0.10 : 0.12),\n'
              '          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(10),\n'
